   Computer bettors
   ===================================================================== */
var STY={fav:{p:.85,tag:'Backs the favorite'},show:{p:.85,tag:'Across the board'},long:{p:.8,tag:'Longshot hunter'},
  exacta:{p:.8,tag:'Exotics player'},whale:{p:.6,tag:'High roller'},gut:{p:.75,tag:'Goes by feel'}};
var ROSTER=[['Dusty','fav','#f2d27e'],['June','show','#93c5fd'],['Pearl','show','#f9a8d4'],['Walt','fav','#a7f3d0'],['Rosa','long','#fca5a5'],
  ['Beau','gut','#fdba74'],['Rusty','long','#fb923c'],['Daisy','gut','#fde047'],['Hank','show','#cbd5e1'],['Earl','whale','#c4b5fd'],
  ['Mabel','exacta','#f0abfc'],['Clem','fav','#86efac'],['Lou','exacta','#94a3b8'],['Cass','gut','#67e8f9'],['Wes','exacta','#fcd34d'],
  ['Ivy','fav','#bef264'],['Marisol','whale','#f472b6'],['Tex','long','#fdba74'],['Duke','whale','#e9d5ff'],['Bianca','gut','#fda4af']];
var BANT={fav:'book',show:'never',long:'hunch',exacta:'counter',whale:'whale',gut:'hunch'};
var bots=[];
function seatBots(n){var have=bots.slice(0,n),pool=ROSTER.filter(function(r){return !have.some(function(b){return b.name===r[0];});});
  while(have.length<n&&pool.length){var r=pool.splice(Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length),1)[0];have.push({id:'bot-'+r[0].toLowerCase(),name:r[0],sty:r[1],col:r[2],key:BANT[r[1]],net:0});}
  bots=have;}
var LINES={
  bet:['{a} on {h} to {t}.','I like {h}. {a} to {t}.','Gimme {h}, {a} to {t}.','{h} has that look today.'],
  ex:['{h} then {h2}. Exacta, {a}.','Calling it: {h} over {h2}.'],
  cheer:['Go {h}, go!','Come on, {h}!','Run, {h}, run!','That\u2019s my horse!','Hold on, {h}!'],
  win:['Cha-ching! {h} paid {w}.','Told y\u2019all. {h}!','{w} in the saddlebag.','Easy money on {h}.'],
  big:['{w}! Drinks are on me!','Holy smokes, {h}! {w}!','Somebody pinch me. {w}!'],
  near:['Second by a nose. Story of my life.','So close! {h} just missed.','Dang it, {h}, one more stride!'],
  long:['A longshot! Who had {h}?','{h} at {o}? Wild!','Nobody saw {h} coming.'],
  bust:['Easy come, easy go.','Next race, partner.','That horse needs glue, not oats. Kidding!']};
var lastTalk=0;
function botSay(b,text,force){if(!opt.talk)return;var now=Date.now();if(!force&&now-lastTalk<2600)return;lastTalk=now;
  showBubble(b.name,text,b.col);try{if(CH)CH.post(b.id,b.name,text,b.col);}catch(e){}}
function botPick(sty,F){var rk=F.rank,pk=pick,shuf=function(n){var a=[1,2,3,4,5,6];for(var i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){var j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1)),x=a[i];a[i]=a[j];a[j]=x;}return a.slice(0,n);};
  if(sty==='fav')return Math.random()<.6?{t:'W',m:'st',s:[rk[0]],u:pk([10,20,50,100])}:{t:'A',m:'st',s:[rk[0]],u:pk([5,10,20])};
  if(sty==='show')return Math.random()<.5?{t:'S',m:'st',s:[pk(rk.slice(0,3))],u:pk([20,50,100])}:{t:'A',m:'st',s:[pk(rk.slice(0,3))],u:pk([2,5,10])};
  if(sty==='long')return Math.random()<.6?{t:'W',m:'st',s:[pk(rk.slice(4))],u:pk([2,5,10])}:{t:'A',m:'st',s:[pk(rk.slice(3))],u:pk([2,5])};
  if(sty==='whale')return Math.random()<.6?{t:'W',m:'st',s:[rk[0]],u:pk([500,1000,2500])}:{t:'P',m:'st',s:[pk(rk.slice(0,2))],u:pk([500,1000])};
  if(sty==='exacta'){var r=Math.random();
    if(r<.2)return {t:'X',m:'box',s:rk.slice(0,3),u:pk([1,2])};
    if(r<.35)return {t:'Q',m:'st',s:[rk[0],rk[1]].sort(),u:pk([2,5])};
    if(r<.55)return {t:'T',m:'box',s:shuf(4).sort(),u:pk([.5,1])};
    if(r<.7)return {t:'T',m:'key',s:[rk[0]].concat(rk.slice(1,4).sort()),u:pk([1,2])};
    if(r<.85)return {t:'F',m:'box',s:shuf(5).sort(),u:pk([.1,.2])};
    return {t:'D',m:'st',s:[rk[0],1+Math.floor(Math.random()*NH)],u:pk([2,5])};}
  var t=pk(['W','P','S','A','A','X','Q','D']),h=shuf(2);
  if(t==='X')return {t:t,m:'st',s:h,u:pk([1,2,5])};if(t==='Q')return {t:t,m:'st',s:h.sort(),u:pk([1,2,5])};
  if(t==='D')return {t:'D',m:'st',s:[h[0],1+Math.floor(Math.random()*NH)],u:pk([1,2])};
  return {t:t,m:'st',s:[h[0]],u:pk([2,5,10,20])};}
function betSay(bet,F){var h=F.h[bet.s[0]-1].name;
  if(bet.t==='A')return pick(['{a} across the board on {h}.','Give me {h} across the board, {a} each.','{h} to finish top three. {a} across.']).replace('{a}',amtTxt(bet.u)).replace('{h}',h);
  if(BT[bet.t].k===1)return pick(LINES.bet).replace('{h}',h).replace('{a}',amtTxt(bet.u)).replace('{t}',{W:'win',P:'place',S:'show'}[bet.t]);
  if(bet.t==='D')return 'Daily Double: '+h+' now, number '+bet.s[1]+' next race.';
  return pick(['Playing a {x}.','{x}. Feeling lucky.','Gimme a {x}, please.']).replace('{x}',ticketTxt({t:bet.t,m:bet.m,s:bet.s,u:bet.u}));}

/* =====================================================================
   Engine: runs the races in solo play and at a live track's host
   ===================================================================== */
var E=null,pubT=0,lastPub=0;
function eStart(ls){
  stopEngine();
  E={id:ls?ls.id:Math.floor(100+Math.random()*900),ph:'bet',pl:{},plan:[],cheers:[],hist:ls&&ls.hist?ls.hist.slice(0,40):HIST.slice(0,40),timer:0};
  if(ls&&(ls.ph==='race'||ls.ph==='result')&&ls.seed){
    /* the old host left: we saw the seed when the gates opened, so the race simply carries on */
    E.ph=ls.ph;E.seed=ls.seed;E.hash=ls.hash;E.F=fieldOf(ls.hash);E.fo=ls.fo||null;E.f=ls.f||0;E.v=0;
    E.be=ls.be+OFF;E.rs=ls.rs+OFF;E.re=ls.re+OFF;E.ce=ls.ce+OFF;E.RM=raceOf(E.seed,E.F,E.fo);
    (ls.pl||[]).forEach(function(p){E.pl[p.i]={id:p.i,name:p.n,k:p.k,col:p.c,b:(p.b||[]).map(function(x){return {d:x.d,t:x.t,m:x.m||'st',s:x.s.slice(),u:x.u,a:x.a,h1:ls.hash};})};});
    publish(true);
  } else eNewRound();
  E.timer=setInterval(eTick,40);
}
function stopEngine(){if(E){clearInterval(E.timer);}E=null;clearTimeout(pubT);pubT=0;}
function eNewRound(){
  var rec=nextSeed(),now=Date.now();
  E.id++;E.ph='bet';E.seed=rec.seed;E.hash=rec.hash;E.v=0;E.f=0;E.fo=null;E.F=fieldOf(E.hash);E.RM=null;
  E.be=now+BET_MS/SP;E.rs=0;E.re=0;E.ce=0;E.cheers=[];
  var ids={};roster().forEach(function(p){ids[p.id]=1;});ids[myId()]=1;
  for(var k in E.pl){var p=E.pl[k];if(p.k==='b'||!ids[k])delete E.pl[k];else p.b=[];}
  if(Math.random()<.12&&bots.length>2&&opt.bots>0){bots.splice(Math.floor(Math.random()*bots.length),1);seatBots(opt.bots);var inn=bots[bots.length-1];
    if(inn&&opt.talk)setTimeout(function(){showBubble(inn.name,pick(['Room at the rail?','Howdy, folks. Who\u2019s running?','Evening. What\u2019s the hot tip?']),inn.col);},1500/SP);}
  E.plan=[];var win=BET_MS/SP;
  bots.forEach(function(b){var s=STY[b.sty];if(Math.random()>s.p)return;var n=1+(Math.random()<.35?1:0)+(Math.random()<.12?1:0);
    for(var j=0;j<n;j++)E.plan.push({b:b,at:now+rr(.05,.92)*win,bet:botPick(b.sty,E.F)});});
  publish(true);
}
function eBet(pid,p,name,kind,col){
  if(!E||E.ph!=='bet'||!p||p.r!==E.id||Date.now()>=E.be)return false;
  var t=String(p.t),m=String(p.m||'st'),s=Array.isArray(p.s)?p.s.map(Number):[],u=Math.round(+p.u*100)/100,d=String(p.d||'').slice(0,40);
  if(!BT[t]||!d||!(u>=BT[t].min-1e-9)||u>1e6||!validSel(t,m,s))return false;
  var a=costOf(t,m,s,u);if(a>1e7)return false;
  var o=E.pl[pid];if(!o)o=E.pl[pid]={id:pid,name:String(name||'Guest').slice(0,18),k:kind||'p',col:col||'',b:[]};
  if(name)o.name=String(name).slice(0,18);
  var ex=o.b.filter(function(x){return x.d===d;})[0];
  if(ex){ex.u=u;ex.a=a;}else{if(o.b.length>=24)return false;o.b.push({d:d,t:t,m:m,s:s,u:u,a:a,h1:E.hash});}
  publish(false);return true;}
function eCancel(pid,p){if(!E||E.ph!=='bet'||!p||p.r!==E.id)return;var o=E.pl[pid];if(!o)return;
  var n=o.b.length;o.b=o.b.filter(function(x){return p.d==='*'?false:x.d!==p.d;});if(o.b.length!==n)publish(false);}
function eStartRace(){
  var now=Date.now();
  if(forceNext){E.fo=forceNext.slice();E.f=1;forceNext=null;}
  E.RM=raceOf(E.seed,E.F,E.fo);E.ph='race';E.rs=now+GATE_MS/SP;E.re=E.rs+(E.RM.tl*1000+TAIL_MS)/SP;E.ce=E.re+POST_MS/SP;E.plan=[];
  /* computer bettors cheer on their horses at the big moments of the call */
  E.cheers=E.RM.cmt.filter(function(c){return c.k==='l'||c.k==='m';}).map(function(c){return {at:E.rs+c.t*1000/SP,c:c};});
  E.cheers.push({at:E.rs+300/SP,c:{k:'go'}});
  publish(true);}
function eTick(){
  if(!E)return;var now=Date.now();
  if(E.ph==='bet'){
    for(var i=E.plan.length-1;i>=0;i--){var q=E.plan[i];if(now>=q.at){E.plan.splice(i,1);if(bots.indexOf(q.b)>=0){
      var d='b'+(++betSeq);if(eBet(q.b.id,{r:E.id,d:d,t:q.bet.t,m:q.bet.m,s:q.bet.s,u:q.bet.u},q.b.name,'b',q.b.col)&&Math.random()<.14)botSay(q.b,betSay(q.bet,E.F));}}}
    if(now>=E.be)eStartRace();
  } else if(E.ph==='race'){
    for(var j=E.cheers.length-1;j>=0;j--){var c=E.cheers[j];if(now>=c.at){E.cheers.splice(j,1);botCheer(c.c);}}
    if(now>=E.re)eFinish();
  } else if(E.ph==='result'){if(now>=E.ce)eNewRound();}
}
function botsOn(n){var out=[];for(var k in E.pl){var o=E.pl[k];if(o.k!=='b')continue;o.b.forEach(function(b){if(b.s[0]===n&&b.t!=='S')out.push(o);});}return out;}
function botCheer(c){if(!opt.talk||!bots.length)return;var bm={};bots.forEach(function(b){bm[b.id]=b;});
  if(c.k==='go'){var all=[];for(var k in E.pl)if(E.pl[k].k==='b'&&E.pl[k].b.length)all.push(E.pl[k]);if(all.length&&Math.random()<.6){var o=pick(all),b=bm[o.id];if(b)botSay(b,pick(LINES.cheer).replace('{h}',E.F.h[o.b[0].s[0]-1].name));}return;}
  var lead=c.h||E.RM&&(function(){var t=(c.t||0),best=1,bx=-1;for(var n=1;n<=NH;n++){var x=posAt(E.RM.H[n-1],t);if(x>bx){bx=x;best=n;}}return best;})();
  var fans=botsOn(lead);if(fans.length&&Math.random()<.55){var f=bm[pick(fans).id];if(f)botSay(f,pick(LINES.cheer).replace('{h}',E.F.h[lead-1].name));}}
function eFinish(){
  E.ph='result';var ord=E.RM.ord,F_=E.F,bm={};bots.forEach(function(b){bm[b.id]=b;});
  var w=F_.h[ord[0]-1];
  E.hist.unshift({id:E.id,w:ord.slice(0,4),nm:w.name,c:w.silk,c2:w.silk2,o:w.frac,lo:w.win>=9,s:E.seed,h:E.hash,f:E.f,fo:E.fo});if(E.hist.length>40)E.hist.length=40;
  publish(true);
  var best=null,near=null,lost=[];
  /* Daily Doubles from the race before are decided now */
  E.ddq=(E.ddq||[]).filter(function(q){if(q.r>=E.id)return true;var b=bm[q.id];var pay=ord[0]===q.s[1]?r2(q.u*ddPrice(fieldOf(q.h1),q.s[0],F_,q.s[1])):0;
    if(b){b.net+=pay;if(pay>0&&(!best||pay>best.pay))best={b:b,x:{t:'D',s:[ord[0]],a:q.u},pay:pay};}return false;});
  for(var k in E.pl){var o=E.pl[k];if(o.k!=='b')continue;var b=bm[o.id];if(!b)continue;
    o.b.forEach(function(x){b.net-=x.a;if(x.t==='D'){if(ord[0]===x.s[0])E.ddq.push({id:o.id,r:E.id,s:x.s,u:x.u,h1:E.hash});return;}
      var pay=payOf(F_,x,ord);b.net+=pay;if(pay>0){if(!best||pay>best.pay)best={b:b,x:x,pay:pay};}
      else{lost.push(b);if((x.t==='W'||x.t==='A')&&ord[1]===x.s[0])near={b:b,x:x};}});}
  setTimeout(function(){if(!opt.talk||!bots.length||!E)return;
    if(w.win>=10&&Math.random()<.7){var by=best&&best.x.s[0]===w.n?best.b:pick(bots);botSay(by,pick(LINES.long).replace('{h}',w.name).replace('{o}',w.frac),true);return;}
    if(best&&(best.pay>=2500||best.pay>=best.x.a*10)&&Math.random()<.9){botSay(best.b,pick(LINES.big).replace('{w}',money(best.pay)).replace('{h}',F_.h[best.x.s[0]-1].name),true);return;}
    if(near&&Math.random()<.6){botSay(near.b,pick(LINES.near).replace('{h}',F_.h[near.x.s[0]-1].name),true);return;}
    if(best&&Math.random()<.5){botSay(best.b,pick(LINES.win).replace('{w}',money(best.pay)).replace('{h}',F_.h[best.x.s[0]-1].name),true);return;}
    if(lost.length&&Math.random()<.25)botSay(pick(lost),pick(LINES.bust),true);},2600/SP);
}
function snapshot(){
  var pl=[];for(var k in E.pl){var o=E.pl[k];pl.push({i:o.id,n:o.name,k:o.k,c:o.col,b:o.b.map(function(b){return {d:b.d,t:b.t,m:b.m,s:b.s,u:b.u,a:b.a};})});}
  var st={id:E.id,ph:E.ph,hash:E.hash,be:E.be,rs:E.rs,re:E.re,ce:E.ce,hn:Date.now(),sp:SP,v:E.v||0,pl:pl,hist:E.hist.slice(0,40)};
  if(E.ph!=='bet'){st.seed=E.seed;st.f=E.f;if(E.fo)st.fo=E.fo;}
  return st;}
function publish(force){
  if(!E)return;
  if(!force){if(pubT)return;var wait=Math.max(0,160-(Date.now()-lastPub));pubT=setTimeout(function(){pubT=0;publish(true);},wait);return;}
  clearTimeout(pubT);pubT=0;lastPub=Date.now();var st=snapshot();
  if(mode==='host'&&L)try{L.send('st',st);}catch(e){}
  applySt(st,myId());
}
function act(type,p){
  if(mode==='view'){if(L)L.send(type,p,hostId());return;}
  if(!E)return;var me=myId();
  if(type==='bet')eBet(me,p,myName(),'p','');else if(type==='cancel')eCancel(me,p);}

/* =====================================================================
   Client: what every browser does with the race state
   ===================================================================== */
function settle(id,F_,ord){
  var tot=0,stake=0,any=false;
  MB.forEach(function(b){if(b.r!==id||b.st!=='live')return;any=true;stake+=b.a;
    if(b.t==='D'){if(ord[0]===b.s[0]){b.st='dd';}else{b.st='lost';b.w=0;}return;}
    var pay=payOf(F_,b,ord);b.w=pay;b.st=pay>0?'won':'lost';bank=r2(bank+pay);tot+=pay;});
  /* Daily Doubles carried over from the race before: leg 2 is this race */
  DDQ.forEach(function(b){if(b.st!=='dd'||b.r>=id)return;any=true;var pay=ord[0]===b.s[1]?r2(b.u*ddPrice(fieldOf(b.h1),b.s[0],F_,b.s[1])):0;
    b.w=pay;b.l2=id;b.st=pay>0?'won':'lost';bank=r2(bank+pay);tot+=pay;});
  return any?{pay:r2(tot),stake:r2(stake)}:null;}
function refund(b,why){if(b.st==='placed'||b.st==='live'||b.st==='dd'){bank=r2(bank+b.a);b.st='void';b.w=b.a;b.why=why||'';}}
function histOrd(hh){var F_=fieldOf(hh.h);return {F:F_,ord:hh.fo&&hh.fo.length===NH?hh.fo:orderOf(hh.s,F_)};}
function applySt(st,from){
  if(!st||typeof st.id!=='number')return;
  if(mode==='view'&&from!==hostId()&&from!==lastFrom)return;
  if(mode!=='view'&&from!==myId())return;
  if(from===myId()){OFF=0;offN=0;}
  else{var smp=Date.now()-st.hn;if(from!==lastFrom||!offN||Math.abs(smp-OFF)>1500){OFF=smp;offN=1;}else{OFF=OFF*.85+smp*.15;offN++;}}
  lastFrom=from;lastSt=st;
  if(Array.isArray(st.hist))setHist(st.hist);
  var newRound=st.id!==R.id;
  if(newRound){
    /* tickets on a race we never saw finish: settle from the history if it ran, otherwise give the stakes back */
    MB.forEach(function(b){if(b.st!=='placed'&&b.st!=='live')return;var hh=HIST.filter(function(x){return x.id===b.r;})[0];
      if(hh&&b.st==='live'&&hh.s){var o=histOrd(hh);if(b.t==='D'){if(o.ord[0]===b.s[0])b.st='dd';else{b.st='lost';b.w=0;}return;}var pay=payOf(o.F,b,o.ord);b.w=pay;b.st=pay>0?'won':'lost';bank=r2(bank+pay);}
      else refund(b,'Race called off: stake returned');});
    DDQ.forEach(function(b){if(b.st!=='dd')return;var hh=HIST.filter(function(x){return x.id>b.r&&x.id<st.id&&x.s;}).pop();
      if(hh){var o=histOrd(hh);var pay=o.ord[0]===b.s[1]?r2(b.u*ddPrice(fieldOf(b.h1),b.s[0],o.F,b.s[1])):0;b.w=pay;b.l2=hh.id;b.st=pay>0?'won':'lost';bank=r2(bank+pay);}});
    var doneDD=DDQ.filter(function(b){return b.st!=='dd';});
    DDQ=DDQ.filter(function(b){return b.st==='dd';}).concat(MB.filter(function(b){return b.st==='dd';}));
    if(MB.length||doneDD.length){PREV=MB.concat(doneDD);var ls=MB.filter(function(b){return b.st!=='void';}).map(function(b){return {t:b.t,m:b.m,s:b.s.slice(),u:b.u};});if(ls.length)LASTSET=ls;}
    MB=[];sel=[];dd2=0;R.verified=null;podHide();
  }
  R.id=st.id;R.ph=st.ph;R.hash=st.hash;R.be=st.be;R.rs=st.rs;R.re=st.re;R.ce=st.ce;R.sp=st.sp||1;R.v=st.v||0;
  if(newRound||!F)F=fieldOf(R.hash);
  if(st.ph!=='bet'&&st.seed){if(R.seed!==st.seed||!RM){R.seed=st.seed;R.f=st.f||0;R.fo=st.fo||null;RM=raceOf(R.seed,F,R.fo);cmtIdx=-1;}}
  else if(st.ph==='bet'){R.seed='';R.f=0;R.fo=null;RM=null;}
  PL=st.pl||[];
  var me=null;PL.forEach(function(p){if(p.i===myId())me=p;});
  if(st.ph==='bet'&&newRound){SFX.bugle();if(!REDUCED)FX.reset();
    if(R.id>=0&&pendingRepeat){pendingRepeat=false;setTimeout(repeatBets,0);}}
  if(st.ph==='race'||st.ph==='result'){
    /* my tickets: did they make post time? */
    MB.forEach(function(b){if(b.r!==R.id||b.st!=='placed')return;var hb=me&&me.b.filter(function(x){return x.d===b.d;})[0];
      if(hb){if(Math.abs(hb.a-b.a)>0.004){bank=r2(bank+b.a-hb.a);b.a=hb.a;b.u=hb.u;}b.st='live';}else{refund(b,'Missed post time: stake returned');note('Missed post time: stake returned','');}});
    if(raceSeen!==R.id&&st.ph==='race'){raceSeen=R.id;FX.load();}
    if(R.seed&&!R.f){var vid=R.id,vh=R.hash;sha(R.seed).then(function(h){if(R.id===vid){R.verified=h===vh;fair();}});}
  }
  if(st.ph==='result'&&settledId!==R.id&&RM){settledId=R.id;var res=settle(R.id,F,RM.ord);showPodium(res);}
  ui();renderBoard();renderSlip();renderPlayers();fair();
}
var pendingRepeat=false;
function normSel(t,m,s){s=s.map(Number);if(m==='box'||t==='Q')s=s.slice().sort(function(a,b){return a-b;});else if(m==='key')s=[s[0]].concat(s.slice(1).sort(function(a,b){return a-b;}));return s;}
function placeBet(t,m,s,u,quiet){
  if(!F||!bettingOpen()){note(R.ph==='bet'?'Betting just closed':'Betting opens after this race','r');return false;}
  m=m||'st';u=Math.round((u||AMT[t])*100)/100;s=normSel(t,m,s);
  if(!validSel(t,m,s)){note(t==='D'?'Pick this race\u2019s winner and a post for the next race':'Pick '+BT[t].k+' horses first','r');return false;}
  if(u<BT[t].min-1e-9){note(BT[t].n+' minimum is '+amtTxt(BT[t].min),'r');return false;}
  var add=costOf(t,m,s,u);if(add>bank+1e-9){note('Not enough chips for a '+m2(add)+' ticket','r');return false;}
  var ex=MB.filter(function(b){return b.r===R.id&&b.st==='placed'&&b.t===t&&b.m===m&&b.s.join()===s.join();})[0];
  if(!ex&&MB.filter(function(b){return b.r===R.id;}).length>=20){note('That\u2019s plenty of tickets for one race','r');return false;}
  bank=r2(bank-add);
  if(ex){ex.u=r2(ex.u+u);ex.a=costOf(t,m,s,ex.u);act('bet',{r:R.id,d:ex.d,t:t,m:m,s:s,u:ex.u});}
  else{ex={d:myId().slice(0,8)+'-'+R.id+'-'+(++betSeq),r:R.id,t:t,m:m,s:s.slice(),u:u,a:add,st:'placed',w:0,h1:R.hash};MB.push(ex);act('bet',{r:R.id,d:ex.d,t:t,m:m,s:s,u:u});}
  SFX.chip();if(!quiet)note(ticketTxt(ex)+' \u00b7 '+m2(ex.a),'g');ui();renderBoard();renderSlip();
  if(mode==='view')renderPlayers();
  return true;}
function cancelBet(d){if(!bettingOpen())return;var b=MB.filter(function(x){return x.d===d&&x.st==='placed'&&x.r===R.id;})[0];if(!b)return;
  bank=r2(bank+b.a);MB.splice(MB.indexOf(b),1);act('cancel',{r:R.id,d:d});SFX.chip();ui();renderBoard();renderSlip();if(mode==='view')renderPlayers();}
function clearBets(){if(!bettingOpen())return;MB.filter(function(b){return b.r===R.id&&b.st==='placed';}).forEach(function(b){cancelBet(b.d);});}
function repeatBets(){if(!LASTSET.length){note('No tickets from the last race','');return;}if(!bettingOpen()){pendingRepeat=true;note('Repeating when betting opens','');return;}
  var tot=0;LASTSET.forEach(function(b){tot+=costOf(b.t,b.m,b.s,b.u);});if(tot>bank+1e-9){note('Not enough chips to repeat','r');return;}
  LASTSET.forEach(function(b){placeBet(b.t,b.m,b.s,b.u,true);});note('Repeated '+LASTSET.length+' ticket'+(LASTSET.length>1?'s':'')+' \u00b7 '+m2(tot),'g');}
function busy(){return MB.some(function(b){return b.st==='placed'||b.st==='live'||b.st==='dd';})||DDQ.some(function(b){return b.st==='dd';});}

/* =====================================================================
   UI: tote board, slip, bettors, history, fairness
   ===================================================================== */
/* amount pills: the base bet for the chosen type (Across = per part) */
var amtKey='';
function renderAmts(){var lad=AL[ty],cur=AMT[ty],i=lad.indexOf(cur);if(i<0){lad=lad.concat([cur]).sort(function(a,b){return a-b;});i=lad.indexOf(cur);}
  var st=Math.max(0,Math.min(i-2,lad.length-5)),set=lad.slice(st,st+5),mult=ty==='A'?3:1;
  var key=ty+'|'+cur+'|'+set.join()+'|'+set.map(function(v){return v*mult>bank+1e-9?1:0;}).join();
  if(key!==amtKey){amtKey=key;$('amts').innerHTML=set.map(function(v){return '<button type="button" data-v="'+v+'"'+(v===cur?' class="sel" aria-pressed="true"':' aria-pressed="false"')+(v*mult>bank+1e-9?' disabled':'')+' aria-label="'+amtTxt(v)+' base">'+amtTxt(v)+'</button>';}).join('');}
  var c=curCost();$('stkL').textContent=exo(ty)||ty==='D'?(c?'Ticket':amtTxt(cur)+' per combo'):ty==='A'?amtTxt(cur)+' \u00d7 3':'Ticket';$('stk').textContent=c?m2(c):'\u2014';}
$('amts').addEventListener('click',function(e){var t=e.target.closest('button');if(!t||t.disabled)return;AMT[ty]=+t.getAttribute('data-v');saveP();SFX.chip();boardKey='';renderBoard();});
function curSel(){return ty==='D'?[sel[0],dd2]:sel.slice();}
function curCost(){if(!exo(ty)&&ty!=='D')return ty==='A'?r2(AMT.A*3):AMT[ty];var m=ty==='D'?'st':bmode,s=curSel();return validSel(ty,m,ty==='D'?s:normSel(ty,m,s))?costOf(ty,m,s,AMT[ty]):0;}
function scHtml(h,cls){return '<span class="sc'+(cls?' '+cls:'')+'" style="--c:'+h.silk+';--c2:'+h.silk2+'">'+h.n+'</span>';}
function myStake(t,n){var a=0;MB.forEach(function(b){if(b.r===R.id&&b.t===t&&BT[t].k===1&&b.s[0]===n&&(b.st==='placed'||b.st==='live'))a+=b.a;});return a;}
var POS=['1st','2nd','3rd','4th'];
function pickLabel(n){var i=sel.indexOf(n);
  if(ty==='D')return sel[0]===n?['Leg 1','p1']:['Pick',''];
  if(bmode==='box')return i>=0?['In box','on']:['Add',''];
  if(bmode==='key')return i===0?['Key','p1']:i>0?['With','on']:[sel.length?'With':'Key',''];
  if(ty==='Q')return i>=0?['Picked','on']:['Pick',''];
  return i>=0?[POS[i],i===0?'p1':'on']:['Pick',''];}
var boardKey='';
function renderBoard(){
  var od=$('pOdds');od.setAttribute('data-ty',ty);od.classList.toggle('exo',exo(ty)||ty==='D');
  if(!BT[ty][bmode]&&bmode!=='st')bmode='st';
  [].forEach.call($('tys').querySelectorAll('button'),function(b){var on=b.getAttribute('data-t')===ty;b.classList.toggle('on',on);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on);});
  if($('tysel').value!==ty)$('tysel').value=ty;
  $('raceLbl').textContent=R.id>=0?'Race '+R.id:'';
  $('help').innerHTML=HELP[ty];
  if(!F){$('orows').innerHTML='<div class="sempty">Saddling up&hellip;</div>';return;}
  var key=R.hash+'|'+ty+'|'+bmode+'|'+sel.join()+'|'+dd2+'|'+AMT[ty]+'|'+MB.map(function(b){return b.d+b.a+b.st;}).join();
  if(key!==boardKey){boardKey=key;var straight=!exo(ty)&&ty!=='D';
    var hl={W:'W',P:'P',S:'S',A:'WPS'}[ty]||'';
    $('ohdAct').textContent=straight?'Bet':'Pick';
    $('orows').innerHTML=F.h.map(function(h){var act,stk=straight?myStake(ty,h.n):0;
      var sm=ty==='P'?'P '+m2(h.p2pay):ty==='S'||ty==='A'?'S '+m2(h.s2pay):'W '+m2(h.w2);
      if(straight)act='<button type="button" class="pb go'+(stk?' has':'')+'" data-h="'+h.n+'" aria-label="'+esc(ticketTxt({t:ty,u:AMT[ty]}))+' on #'+h.n+' '+esc(h.name)+'"><b>'+amtTxt(AMT[ty])+' '+BT[ty].sh+'</b><i>'+(stk?'on it '+m2(stk):ty==='A'?m2(AMT.A*3)+' ticket':'')+'</i></button>';
      else{var pl=pickLabel(h.n);act='<button type="button" class="pb pk '+pl[1]+'" data-h="'+h.n+'" aria-label="'+pl[0]+' #'+h.n+' '+esc(h.name)+'"><b>'+pl[0]+'</b></button>';}
      return '<div class="or" data-h="'+h.n+'">'+scHtml(h)+'<div class="hn"><b>'+esc(h.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(h.jockey)+' &middot; <i>'+h.form+'</i></small></div>'+
        '<span class="oc"><b>'+h.frac+'</b><small>'+sm+'</small></span>'+
        '<span class="tp'+(hl.indexOf('W')>=0?' hl':'')+'">'+m2(h.w2)+'</span><span class="tp'+(hl.indexOf('P')>=0?' hl':'')+'">'+m2(h.p2pay)+'</span><span class="tp'+(hl.indexOf('S')>=0?' hl':'')+'">'+m2(h.s2pay)+'</span>'+act+'</div>';}).join('');
    renderExbar();}
  renderAmts();lockUI();}
function renderExbar(){if(!(exo(ty)||ty==='D'))return;var m=ty==='D'?'st':bmode,s=curSel(),k=BT[ty].k,base=baseU(ty),h='';
  if(ty!=='D'){var opts=[['st',ty==='Q'?'Pick 2':'Straight']];if(BT[ty].box)opts.push(['box','Box']);if(BT[ty].key)opts.push(['key','Key']);
    h+='<div class="mseg" role="group" aria-label="Straight, box or key">'+opts.map(function(o){return '<button type="button" data-m="'+o[0]+'" class="'+(o[0]===bmode?'on':'')+'">'+o[1]+'</button>';}).join('')+'</div>';}
  else h+='<div class="ddpad" role="group" aria-label="Next race winner"><span>Next race</span>'+[1,2,3,4,5,6].map(function(n){return '<button type="button" data-n="'+n+'" class="'+(dd2===n?'on':'')+'" aria-label="Next race post '+n+'">'+n+'</button>';}).join('')+'</div>';
  var txt,ok=ty==='D'?validSel('D','st',s):validSel(ty,m,normSel(ty,m,s)),cost=ok?costOf(ty,m,s,AMT[ty]):0;
  if(ty==='D'){var h1=sel[0]?F.h[sel[0]-1]:null;
    txt=!h1?'Tap the horse you think wins <em>this</em> race, then pick a post for the next race.':!dd2?'Leg 1: #'+h1.n+' '+esc(h1.name)+' ('+h1.frac+'). Now pick a post for the next race.':
      '#'+h1.n+' now, #'+dd2+' next race. Pays this horse\u2019s win price \u00d7 the leg-2 winner\u2019s win price \u00f7 0.92, e.g. <em>'+m2(Math.round(h1.win*3/RTP*100)/100*AMT.D)+'</em> if #'+dd2+' wins at 2-1.';}
  else if(!ok){var need=m==='st'?k-s.length:Math.max(0,(m==='key'?k:k)-s.length);
    txt=m==='box'?'Tap '+(need>0?'at least '+need+' more':'more')+' horses to box them (any order).':m==='key'?(s.length?'Key #'+s[0]+'. Now add at least '+need+' horses to finish under it.':'Tap your key horse to win first.'):
      (ty==='Q'?'Tap the two horses you think finish top two, either order.':'Tap '+need+' more: '+POS.slice(s.length,k).join(', then ')+'.');}
  else{var cs=combos(ty,m,normSel(ty,m,s)),n=cs.length,lo=1e9,hi=0;cs.forEach(function(c){var v=exoPrice(F,ty,c);if(v<lo)lo=v;if(v>hi)hi=v;});
    txt=ticketTxt({t:ty,m:m,s:normSel(ty,m,s),u:AMT[ty]})+(n>1?' \u00b7 '+n+' combos \u00d7 '+amtTxt(AMT[ty])+' = <em>'+m2(cost)+'</em>':'')+' \u00b7 '+amtTxt(base)+' pays <em>'+m2(lo*base)+(n>1&&hi>lo?'\u2013'+m2(hi*base):'')+'</em>';}
  h+='<span class="ext">'+txt+'</span><button type="button" class="btn" id="exGo"'+(ok&&bettingOpen()&&cost<=bank+1e-9?'':' disabled')+'>'+(ok?'Bet '+m2(cost):'Bet')+'</button>';
  $('exbar').innerHTML=h;}

  if(!open){var txt=R.ph==='bet'?'Betting closed<small>They\u2019re heading to the gate</small>':R.ph==='race'?'Race '+R.id+' is running<small>Betting opens after the finish</small>':R.ph==='result'?'Official results<small>Next race in '+Math.max(0,Math.ceil((R.ce-hn)/1000))+'s</small>':'Saddling up<small>Waiting for the track</small>';
    if($('lockTxt').innerHTML!==txt)$('lockTxt').innerHTML=txt;}}
function tapPick(n){
  if(ty==='D'){sel=sel[0]===n?[]:[n];}
  else{var i=sel.indexOf(n),k=BT[ty].k;
    if(bmode==='box'){if(i>=0)sel.splice(i,1);else if(sel.length<NH)sel.push(n);}
    else if(bmode==='key'){if(i>=0)sel.splice(i,1);else sel.push(n);}
    else{if(i>=0)sel.splice(i,1);else if(sel.length<k)sel.push(n);else sel[k-1]=n;}}
  SFX.tick();boardKey='';renderBoard();}

/* the bet slip: tickets like the window prints them */
function lastHashOf(id){if(id===R.id)return R.hash;var h=HIST.filter(function(x){return x.id===id;})[0];return h?h.h:R.hash;}
function maxPay(b,F_){if(!F_)return 0;if(b.t==='A'){var h=F_.h[b.s[0]-1];return r2(b.u*(h.win+h.place+h.show));}if(BT[b.t].k===1){var x=F_.h[b.s[0]-1];return r2(b.u*(b.t==='W'?x.win:b.t==='P'?x.place:x.show));}
  if(b.t==='D')return 0;var hi=0;combos(b.t,b.m,b.s).forEach(function(c){var v=exoPrice(F_,b.t,c);if(v>hi)hi=v;});return r2(b.u*hi);}
function slipRow(b){var F_=fieldOf(b.h1||lastHashOf(b.r)),cls=b.st==='won'?' won':b.st==='lost'?' lost':b.st==='void'?' void':b.st==='dd'?' dd':'';
  var bs=(BT[b.t].k===1||b.t==='D'?[b.s[0]]:b.s.slice(0,3)).map(function(n){return scHtml(F_.h[n-1]);}).join('')+(b.t!=='D'&&b.s.length>3?'<span class="more">+'+(b.s.length-3)+'</span>':'');
  var sub='Race '+b.r+(b.t==='D'?'\u2013'+(b.l2||b.r+1):'')+' \u00b7 ';
  if(BT[b.t].k===1)sub+='#'+b.s[0]+' '+esc(F_.h[b.s[0]-1].name)+(b.t==='A'?' \u00b7 '+m2(b.a)+' ticket':'');
  else if(b.t==='D')sub+='#'+b.s[0]+' '+esc(F_.h[b.s[0]-1].name)+', then post '+b.s[1];
  else{var n=nComb(b.t,b.m,b.s);sub+=(n>1?n+' combos \u00b7 ':'')+m2(b.a);}
  var right;
  if(b.st==='won'){var nt=r2(b.w-b.a);right='<b>'+(nt<0?'\u2212':'+')+m2(Math.abs(nt))+'</b><small>paid '+m2(b.w)+'</small>';}
  else if(b.st==='lost')right='<b>'+'\u2212'+m2(b.a)+'</b><small>'+(b.t==='D'&&b.l2?'leg 2 missed':'no luck')+'</small>';
  else if(b.st==='void')right='<b>'+m2(b.a)+'</b><small>returned</small>';
  else if(b.st==='dd')right='<b>'+m2(b.a)+'</b><small>leg 1 \u2713 \u00b7 pending</small>';
  else{var mp=maxPay(b,F_);right='<b>'+m2(b.a)+'</b><small>'+(b.t==='D'?'pending':(mp?(BT[b.t].k>1||b.t==='A'?'up to ':'pays ')+m2(mp):''))+'</small>';}
  var stt=b.st==='live'?' \u00b7 running':b.st==='dd'?' \u00b7 waiting on Race '+(b.r+1):'';
  return '<div class="sr'+cls+'"><span class="bs">'+bs+'</span><div class="sd"><b>'+esc(ticketTxt(b))+'</b><small>'+sub+stt+(b.why?' \u00b7 '+esc(b.why):'')+'</small></div>'+
    '<div class="sa">'+right+'</div><button type="button" class="sx" data-d="'+esc(b.d)+'"'+(b.st==='placed'&&bettingOpen()&&b.r===R.id?'':' disabled')+' aria-label="Pull this ticket">\u00d7</button></div>';}
var slipKey='';
function renderSlip(){
  var cur=MB.filter(function(b){return b.r===R.id;}),pend=DDQ.concat(MB.filter(function(b){return b.st==='dd'&&b.r!==R.id;})),open=bettingOpen();
  var key=R.id+'|'+R.ph+'|'+open+'|'+cur.concat(pend,PREV).map(function(b){return b.d+b.a+b.st;}).join();
  var n=cur.length,tot=0,maxR=0;cur.forEach(function(b){tot+=b.a;if(b.st!=='void')maxR+=maxPay(b,F);});
  $('tabSlip').textContent=n+pend.length?String(n+pend.length):'';$('slipN').textContent=n+(n===1?' ticket':' tickets');$('slipTot').textContent=m2(tot);
  $('rptBtn').disabled=!LASTSET.length;$('clrBtn').disabled=!open||!cur.some(function(b){return b.st==='placed';});
  if(key===slipKey)return;slipKey=key;
  var h='';
  if(cur.length)h+='<div class="shead">Race '+R.id+'</div>'+cur.map(slipRow).join('');
  if(pend.length)h+='<div class="shead">Daily Double: carried over</div>'+pend.map(slipRow).join('');
  if(!cur.length&&PREV.length)h+='<div class="shead">Last race</div>'+PREV.map(slipRow).join('');
  if(!h)h='<div class="sempty">No tickets yet. Choose a bet type, an amount, then tap a horse.<br><b>Show</b> and <b>Across the Board</b> pay if your horse finishes in the top 3.</div>';
  $('slist').innerHTML=h;
  var sum='';if(cur.length){sum='Staked <b>'+m2(tot)+'</b>'+(maxR?' &middot; best case <b>'+m2(r2(maxR))+'</b>':'');}
  else if(PREV.length){var w=0,s=0;PREV.forEach(function(b){if(b.st==='won')w+=b.w;if(b.st!=='void')s+=b.a;});sum='Last race: staked <b>'+m2(s)+'</b>, paid <b>'+m2(r2(w))+'</b>';}
  $('slipSum').innerHTML=sum;}

function renderPlayers(){
  var list=$('plist'),me=myId(),rows=[],tot=0,res=R.ph==='result'&&RM;
  PL.forEach(function(p){(p.b||[]).forEach(function(b){rows.push({key:p.i+':'+b.d,p:p,b:b,me:p.i===me});tot+=b.a;});});
  if(mode==='view'){MB.forEach(function(b){if(b.r===R.id&&b.st==='placed'&&!rows.some(function(r){return r.me&&r.b.d===b.d;})){rows.push({key:me+':'+b.d,p:{i:me,n:myName(),k:'p'},b:b,me:true});tot+=b.a;}});}
  rows.forEach(function(r){r.dd=res&&r.b.t==='D'&&RM.ord[0]===r.b.s[0];r.pay=res?payOf(F,r.b,RM.ord):0;});
  rows.sort(function(a,b){if(a.me!==b.me)return a.me?-1:1;if(res&&(a.pay>0||a.dd)!==(b.pay>0||b.dd))return a.pay>0||a.dd?-1:1;if((a.p.k==='b')!==(b.p.k==='b'))return a.p.k==='b'?1:-1;return (res?b.pay-a.pay:0)||b.b.a-a.b.a||(a.key<b.key?-1:1);});
  var seen={};
  rows.forEach(function(r){seen[r.key]=1;var el=ROWS[r.key];
    if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.className='pr';el.innerHTML='<span class="av"></span><span class="nm"></span><span class="pk"></span><span class="pb2"></span><span class="pw"></span>';ROWS[r.key]=el;}
    var nm=r.me?(mode==='solo'?'You':r.p.n+' (you)'):r.p.n,av=el.children[0],col=r.p.c||('hsl('+hue(r.p.i)+',70%,64%)');
    if(el.children[1].textContent!==nm)el.children[1].textContent=nm;
    if(av.textContent!==nm.charAt(0).toUpperCase())av.textContent=nm.charAt(0).toUpperCase();if(!av.classList.contains('hasface'))av.style.background=col;
    var pk=F?scHtml(F.h[r.b.s[0]-1])+' '+esc(pickShort(r.b)):'';if(el.children[2].innerHTML!==pk)el.children[2].innerHTML=pk;
    el.children[3].textContent=kmoney(r.b.a);
    var won=res&&r.pay>0,lost=res&&!won&&!r.dd;
    el.children[4].textContent=won?(r.pay<r.b.a?'\u2212'+kmoney(r2(r.b.a-r.pay)):'+'+kmoney(r2(r.pay-r.b.a))):r.dd?'leg 1 \u2713':lost?'\u2212'+kmoney(r.b.a):'';
    el.className='pr'+(r.me?' me':'')+(won||r.dd?' won':'')+(lost?' lost':'');
    list.appendChild(el);});
  for(var k in ROWS)if(!seen[k]){if(ROWS[k].parentNode)ROWS[k].parentNode.removeChild(ROWS[k]);delete ROWS[k];}
  var em=list.querySelector('.pempty');if(!rows.length){if(!em){em=document.createElement('div');em.className='pempty';list.appendChild(em);}em.textContent=R.ph==='bet'?'The windows are open. Be the first to bet.':'No tickets on this race.';}else if(em)em.remove();
  var np={};rows.forEach(function(r){np[r.p.i]=1;});var npn=Object.keys(np).length;
  $('pCount').textContent=npn+(npn===1?' bettor':' bettors');$('pTot').textContent=kmoney(tot)+' wagered';$('tabBet').textContent=npn?String(npn):'';}

var selRound=null;
function short(h){return h?h.slice(0,10)+'\u2026'+h.slice(-6):'';}
function fair(){
  var hl=$('hashln');
  if(R.id<0)hl.textContent='Preparing the hash chain\u2026';
  else if(R.seed)hl.textContent='Race '+R.id+' \u00b7 seed '+short(R.seed)+(R.verified===true?' \u2713':'');
  else hl.textContent='Race '+R.id+' \u00b7 hash '+short(R.hash);
  if(!$('info').classList.contains('on'))return;
  $('fNow').innerHTML='<div><i>This race</i> #'+R.id+(R.ph==='result'?' (official)':R.ph==='race'?' (running)':' (betting)')+'</div><div><i>Hash</i> <span class="fx">'+esc(R.hash||'\u2014')+'</span></div>'+
    (R.seed?'<div><i>Seed</i> <span class="fx">'+esc(R.seed)+'</span>'+(R.verified===true?' <b style="color:#34d399">\u2713</b>':'')+'</div>':'<div><i>Seed</i> revealed when the gates open</div>');
  var r=selRound!==null?HIST.filter(function(x){return x.id===selRound;})[0]:null;if(!r)r=HIST[0];
  $('fLast').innerHTML=r?'<div><i>Checking</i> race #'+r.id+' &middot; won by <b>#'+r.w[0]+' '+esc(r.nm)+'</b> at '+r.o+', then #'+r.w.slice(1).join(', #')+(r.f?' (test race)':'')+'</div><div><i>Seed</i> <span class="fx">'+esc(r.s||'\u2014')+'</span></div><div><i>Hash</i> <span class="fx">'+esc(r.h||'')+'</span></div>':'<div>No finished races yet.</div>';
  $('verBtn').disabled=!r||!r.s;
  oddsTable();menuTable();}
function verifyRound(r){if(!r||!r.s)return Promise.resolve(null);return sha(r.s).then(function(h){var F_=fieldOf(r.h),o=orderOf(r.s,F_);return {hashOk:h===r.h,ord:o,ordOk:o.slice(0,r.w.length).join()===r.w.join(),hash:h};});}
$('verBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){var r=selRound!==null?HIST.filter(function(x){return x.id===selRound;})[0]:null;if(!r)r=HIST[0];var o=$('verRes');
  verifyRound(r).then(function(v){if(!v){o.textContent='';return;}var ok=v.hashOk&&v.ordOk;o.className='vres '+(ok?'ok':'bad');
    o.textContent=ok?'\u2713 Verified: SHA-256(seed) matches the hash shown before betting, and the seed gives #'+v.ord.join(', #')+'.':(r.f?'Test race: the finishing order was set by hand, so it does not match the seed.':'\u2717 Mismatch: hash '+(v.hashOk?'ok':'differs')+', seed gives #'+v.ord.join(', #')+'.');});});
$('seedGo').addEventListener('click',function(){var s=String($('seedIn').value).trim().toLowerCase(),o=$('seedRes');if(!/^[0-9a-f]{40,}$/.test(s)){o.className='vres bad';o.textContent='A seed is a long hex string (0-9, a-f).';return;}
  sha(s).then(function(h){var F_=fieldOf(h),ord=orderOf(s,F_);o.className='vres ok';o.innerHTML='SHA-256: <span class="fx">'+h+'</span><br>That hash fields '+F_.h.map(function(x){return '#'+x.n+' '+esc(x.name)+' ('+x.frac+')';}).join(', ')+'.<br>Finishing order from this seed: <b>'+ord.map(function(n){return '#'+n+' '+esc(F_.h[n-1].name);}).join(', ')+'</b>';});});
$('seedIn').addEventListener('keydown',function(e){e.stopPropagation();});

  if(!SIMR)setTimeout(function(){if(!SIMR){SIMR=sim(20000);SIMR.quick=1;menuKey='';menuTable();}},60);}

var oddsKey='';
function oddsTable(){if(!F)return;if(oddsKey===R.hash)return;oddsKey=R.hash;var pc=function(x){return (x*100).toFixed(1)+'%';};
  var h='<tr><th>Race '+R.id+'</th><th>Odds</th><th>$2 Win</th><th>$2 Place</th><th>$2 Show</th><th>Wins</th><th>Top 2</th><th>Top 3</th></tr>';

  h+='<tr><td>This race returns</td><td class="g" colspan="2">Win '+pc(F.book)+'</td><td class="g">'+pc(rp/NH)+'</td><td class="g">'+pc(rs/NH)+'</td><td colspan="3"></td></tr>';
  $('oddsT').innerHTML=h;}
var SIMR=null,menuKey='';
var MENU=[['W','Win','$2','1st','win'],['P','Place','$2','1st or 2nd','place'],['S','Show','$2','top 3','show'],['A','Across the Board','$6 ($2 each)','top 3, every part that hits','across'],
  ['X','Exacta','$1','1st & 2nd in order','exacta'],['XB','Exacta Box','$1 \u00d7 combos','1st & 2nd any order','exactaBox'],['Q','Quinella','$1','top 2 either order','quinella'],['QB','Quinella Box','$1 \u00d7 pairs','any two of your horses top 2','quinellaBox'],
  ['T','Trifecta','$0.50','top 3 in order','trifecta'],['TB','Trifecta Box','$0.50 \u00d7 combos','top 3 any order','trifectaBox'],['TK','Trifecta Key','$0.50 \u00d7 combos','key wins, others 2nd & 3rd','trifectaKey'],
  ['F','Superfecta','$0.10','top 4 in order','superfecta'],['FB','Superfecta Box','$0.10 \u00d7 combos','top 4 any order','superfectaBox'],['D','Daily Double','$1','winners of 2 races in a row','dailyDouble']];

    (SIMR?'<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:left;color:#94a3b8;white-space:normal">'+(SIMR.quick?'Quick check: ':'')+fmt(SIMR.races)+' simulated races, every combination of the straight bets, random boxes and keys, Daily Doubles over back-to-back races.</td></tr>':'');}
/* the long-run check: many races with every bet type */
function sim(n){n=Math.max(1000,Math.min(2e6,n|0||200000));
  var K=['win','place','show','across','exacta','exactaBox','quinella','quinellaBox','trifecta','trifectaBox','trifectaKey','superfecta','superfectaBox','dailyDouble'],R_={},S_={};K.forEach(function(k){R_[k]=0;S_[k]=0;});
  var a=new Uint32Array(16),pF=null,pW=0,fav=0,favW=0,lngW=0;
  function shuf(k){var x=[1,2,3,4,5,6];for(var i=5;i>0;i--){var j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1)),t=x[i];x[i]=x[j];x[j]=t;}return x.slice(0,k);}
  for(var i=0;i<n;i++){crypto.getRandomValues(a);var hx='',sd='';for(var j=0;j<8;j++){hx+=('00000000'+a[j].toString(16)).slice(-8);sd+=('00000000'+a[j+8].toString(16)).slice(-8);}
    var F_=fieldOf(hx,1),o=orderOf(sd,F_);
    for(var h=0;h<NH;h++){var x=F_.h[h],n_=x.n,pw=o[0]===n_?x.win:0,pp=o[0]===n_||o[1]===n_?x.place:0,ps=o[0]===n_||o[1]===n_||o[2]===n_?x.show:0;
      S_.win++;S_.place++;S_.show++;S_.across+=3;R_.win+=pw;R_.place+=pp;R_.show+=ps;R_.across+=pw+pp+ps;}
    S_.exacta+=30;R_.exacta+=exoPrice(F_,'X',o.slice(0,2));S_.quinella+=15;R_.quinella+=exoPrice(F_,'Q',o.slice(0,2));
    S_.trifecta+=120;R_.trifecta+=exoPrice(F_,'T',o.slice(0,3));S_.superfecta+=360;R_.superfecta+=exoPrice(F_,'F',o.slice(0,4));
    var bx=shuf(3);S_.exactaBox+=6;R_.exactaBox+=payOf(F_,{t:'X',m:'box',s:bx,u:1},o);
    bx=shuf(3);S_.quinellaBox+=3;R_.quinellaBox+=payOf(F_,{t:'Q',m:'box',s:bx,u:1},o);
    bx=shuf(4);S_.trifectaBox+=24;R_.trifectaBox+=payOf(F_,{t:'T',m:'box',s:bx,u:1},o);
    bx=shuf(4);S_.trifectaKey+=6;R_.trifectaKey+=payOf(F_,{t:'T',m:'key',s:bx,u:1},o);
    bx=shuf(5);S_.superfectaBox+=120;R_.superfectaBox+=payOf(F_,{t:'F',m:'box',s:bx,u:1},o);
    if(pF){S_.dailyDouble+=36;R_.dailyDouble+=ddPrice(pF,pW,F_,o[0]);}pF=F_;pW=o[0];
    var fv=F_.rank[0],lg=F_.rank[NH-1];fav++;if(o[0]===fv)favW+=F_.h[fv-1].win;if(o[0]===lg)lngW+=F_.h[lg-1].win;}
  var p=function(x){return Math.round(x*100000)/1000;},rtp={};K.forEach(function(k){rtp[k]=p(R_[k]/S_[k]);});
  return {races:n,rtp:rtp,favoriteWin:p(favW/fav),longshotWin:p(lngW/fav)};}
$('simBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){var o=$('simRes');o.className='vres';o.textContent='Running 200,000 races\u2026';setTimeout(function(){var r=sim(200000);SIMR=r;menuKey='';menuTable();o.className='vres ok';
  o.textContent='200,000 races: '+MENU.map(function(m){return m[1]+' '+r.rtp[m[4]].toFixed(1)+'%';}).join(' \u00b7 ')+' (always backing the favorite to win: '+r.favoriteWin.toFixed(1)+'%, the longest shot: '+r.longshotWin.toFixed(1)+'%)';},40);});
/* bubbles over the track */
function showBubble(name,text,col){var box=$('bubs'),d=document.createElement('div');d.className='bub';
  d.innerHTML='<span class="av"></span><span><span class="nm"></span><span class="tx"></span></span>';d.querySelector('.av').textContent=name.charAt(0);d.querySelector('.av').style.background=col||'#f2d27e';
  d.querySelector('.nm').textContent=name;d.querySelector('.tx').textContent=text;box.appendChild(d);while(box.children.length>2)box.removeChild(box.firstChild);
  setTimeout(function(){d.classList.add('out');setTimeout(function(){d.remove();},450);},3400);}
/* official results chart */
function trophySvg(){if(TROPHY)return '<img class="tro" alt="" src="'+esc(TROPHY)+'">';
  return '<svg class="tro" viewBox="0 0 64 64" aria-hidden="true"><defs><linearGradient id="tg" x1="0" x2="0" y1="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fde68a"/><stop offset=".6" stop-color="#f2d27e"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#a16207"/></linearGradient></defs><path d="M18 8h28v14c0 9-6 16-14 16s-14-7-14-16z" fill="url(#tg)" stroke="#7c5a1e" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M18 12H9c0 9 4 14 10 15M46 12h9c0 9-4 14-10 15" fill="none" stroke="#f2d27e" stroke-width="4"/><rect x="28" y="38" width="8" height="10" fill="#c6a75e"/><rect x="20" y="48" width="24" height="8" rx="2" fill="#7c5a1e"/></svg>';}
function officialPays(F_,o){var r={ex:exoPrice(F_,'X',o.slice(0,2)),qu:exoPrice(F_,'Q',o.slice(0,2)),tri:exoPrice(F_,'T',o.slice(0,3)),sup:exoPrice(F_,'F',o.slice(0,4)),dd:0,ddp:0};
  var prev=HIST.filter(function(x){return x.id<R.id&&x.s;})[0];if(prev){r.ddp=prev.w[0];r.dd=ddPrice(fieldOf(prev.h),prev.w[0],F_,o[0]);}return r;}
var podT=0;
function showPodium(res){if(!RM||!F)return;var o=RM.ord,h=function(i){return F.h[o[i]-1];},P_=officialPays(F,o);
  var row=function(i){var x=h(i);return '<tr'+(i===0?' class="w"':'')+'><td class="ps">'+(i+1)+'</td><td class="h">'+scHtml(x)+esc(x.name)+'</td><td>'+(i===0?m2(x.w2):'')+'</td><td>'+(i<2?m2(x.p2pay):'')+'</td><td>'+(i<3?m2(x.s2pay):'')+'</td></tr>';};
  var html='<h4>'+trophySvg()+'<span>'+(RM.gap<0.06?'Photo! ':'')+'Official &middot; Race '+R.id+(R.f?' (test)':'')+'</span></h4>'+
    '<table class="rc"><tr><th></th><th style="text-align:left">Horse</th><th>$2 Win</th><th>$2 Place</th><th>$2 Show</th></tr>'+row(0)+row(1)+row(2)+row(3)+'</table>'+
    '<div class="exl"><span>$1 Exacta '+o[0]+'-'+o[1]+'<b>'+m2(P_.ex)+'</b></span><span>$1 Quinella '+Math.min(o[0],o[1])+'-'+Math.max(o[0],o[1])+'<b>'+m2(P_.qu)+'</b></span>'+
    '<span>$0.50 Trifecta '+o.slice(0,3).join('-')+'<b>'+m2(r2(P_.tri*.5))+'</b></span><span>$0.10 Superfecta '+o.slice(0,4).join('-')+'<b>'+m2(r2(P_.sup*.1))+'</b></span>'+
    (P_.dd?'<span>$1 Daily Double '+P_.ddp+'-'+o[0]+'<b>'+m2(P_.dd)+'</b></span>':'')+'</div><div class="pyou" id="pyou"></div>';
  $('pod').innerHTML=html;var y=$('pyou');
  if(res){var net=r2(res.pay-res.stake),pend=MB.filter(function(b){return b.st==='dd';}).length;y.className='pyou '+(net>0?'g':net<0&&!pend?'r':'');
    y.textContent=(res.pay>0?'You collect '+m2(res.pay)+' ('+smoney(net)+')':'Your tickets: '+smoney(-res.stake))+(pend?' \u00b7 Daily Double alive for next race':'');
    if(res.pay>0)SFX.win(net>=500);
    try{if(window.suBanner&&net>0&&(net>=Math.max(250,res.stake*5)))suBanner(net>=Math.max(2000,res.stake*15)?'Huge Win!':'Big Win!','+'+money(net)+' on Race '+R.id,2600);}catch(e){}}
  else{y.className='pyou';y.textContent='Winner: #'+o[0]+' '+h(0).name+' at '+h(0).frac;}
  clearTimeout(podT);var wait=Math.max(0,(R.re-hNow())+1700/R.sp);podT=setTimeout(function(){if(R.ph==='result')$('pod').classList.add('on');},wait);}
function podHide(){clearTimeout(podT);$('pod').classList.remove('on');}

/* =====================================================================
   Sound
   ===================================================================== */
var AC=null,NOISE=null;function ac(){if(!opt.sound)return null;try{if(!AC)AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(AC.state==='suspended')AC.resume();}catch(e){}return AC;}
function noise(a){if(NOISE)return NOISE;var b=a.createBuffer(1,a.sampleRate*1.2,a.sampleRate),d=b.getChannelData(0);for(var i=0;i<d.length;i++)d[i]=Math.random()*2-1;NOISE=b;return b;}
function tone(f,t0,dur,type,vol,f2){var a=AC;if(!a||!opt.sound||away)return;var o=a.createOscillator(),g=a.createGain(),t=a.currentTime+(t0||0);o.type=type||'triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f,t);if(f2)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f2,t+dur);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol||.1,t+.015);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);o.connect(g);g.connect(a.destination);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+.05);}
function burst(dur,vol,freq,t0,q){var a=AC;if(!a||!opt.sound||away)return;var s=a.createBufferSource(),f=a.createBiquadFilter(),g=a.createGain(),t=a.currentTime+(t0||0);s.buffer=noise(a);f.type=q?'bandpass':'lowpass';f.frequency.value=freq||1200;if(q)f.Q.value=q;
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol||.2,t+Math.min(.02,dur/3));g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(a.destination);s.start(t);s.stop(t+dur+.05);}
var SFX={chip:function(){tone(1500,0,.05,'square',.04);},
  tick:function(){tone(1200,0,.05,'square',.03);},
  bugle:function(){if(!AC)return;[[523,0,.16],[659,.18,.16],[784,.36,.16],[1047,.54,.4],[784,.98,.14],[1047,1.14,.5]].forEach(function(n){tone(n[0],n[1],n[2],'sawtooth',.035);tone(n[0]*2,n[1],n[2],'triangle',.02);});},
  bell:function(){if(!AC)return;for(var i=0;i<10;i++)tone(1850,i*.07,.09,'square',.03);},
  hoof:function(v){burst(.06,.05*v,380,0,1.2);burst(.05,.04*v,300,.07,1.2);},
  roar:function(){if(!AC)return;burst(2.6,.12,900,0,.5);burst(2.2,.07,2400,.2,.6);},
  win:function(big){[784,988,1319].concat(big?[1568,1976]:[]).forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,i*.07,.35,'triangle',.08);});}};

/* =====================================================================
   Drawing: desert sky, grandstand, rails, the dirt, six runners
   ===================================================================== */
function loadImg(u){var o={ok:false,img:null};if(!u)return o;var im=new Image();im.crossOrigin='anonymous';im.onload=function(){o.ok=true;};im.onerror=function(){o.ok=false;};im.src=u;o.img=im;return o;}
var IM={horse:loadImg(HORSE_SHEET),silk:loadImg(SILK_MASK),bg:loadImg(TRACK_BG),stand:loadImg(GRANDSTAND),dirt:loadImg(DIRT_TILE),gate:loadImg(GATE),post:loadImg(FINISH_POST)};
var cv=$('cv'),cx=cv.getContext('2d'),W=0,H=0,DPR=1,G=null,SKY=null,STAND=null,DIRT=null,LAY=null;
function resize(){var r=$('track').getBoundingClientRect();var w=Math.max(10,Math.round(r.width)),h=Math.max(10,Math.round(r.height));DPR=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  if(w===W&&h===H&&cv.width===Math.round(w*DPR))return;W=w;H=h;cv.width=Math.round(w*DPR);cv.height=Math.round(h*DPR);layout();buildArt();}
function layout(){var narrow=W<560;
  G={narrow:narrow,yStand:H*.15,yRailF:H*.40,y0:H*.475,y1:H*.875,yRailN:H*.94,top:22};
  G.sp=(G.y1-G.y0)/(NH-1);G.hl=Math.min(G.sp*2.55,W*(narrow?.19:.15),210);G.anchor=W*(narrow?.62:.64);G.anchorB=W*(narrow?.8:.74);G.kT=G.hl/9;G.P=Math.max(2.2,H/120);
  G.colW=narrow?30:Math.min(150,Math.max(110,W*.1));}
function buildArt(){
  /* sky + sun */
  SKY=document.createElement('canvas');SKY.width=cv.width;SKY.height=Math.round(G.yRailF*DPR)+2;var g=SKY.getContext('2d');g.scale(DPR,DPR);
  var hz=G.yRailF,gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,hz);gr.addColorStop(0,'#3d7fb8');gr.addColorStop(.45,'#7fb3d5');gr.addColorStop(.8,'#f0cf98');gr.addColorStop(1,'#f6b878');g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,W,hz+2);
  var sx=W*.18,sy=hz*.42,sr=Math.max(14,H*.05),sg=g.createRadialGradient(sx,sy,sr*.3,sx,sy,sr*4);sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,244,200,.9)');sg.addColorStop(.35,'rgba(255,220,150,.3)');sg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,200,120,0)');
  g.fillStyle=sg;g.fillRect(0,0,W,hz);g.fillStyle='#fff6d8';g.beginPath();g.arc(sx,sy,sr,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
  for(var i=0;i<5;i++){var cxp=((i*0.23+0.1)%1)*W,cyp=hz*(.12+(i%3)*.08),cw=W*.08+(i%2)*W*.05;g.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.55)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(cxp,cyp,cw,cw*.16,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.ellipse(cxp+cw*.35,cyp-cw*.06,cw*.5,cw*.15,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}
  LAY=[mkLayer(1,hz*.86,hz*.22,'#c98f6a',.05),mkLayer(2,hz*.94,hz*.16,'#a8664a',.12)];
  /* grandstand strip (tileable) */
  var tw=Math.max(600,Math.round(W*1.2)),th=Math.round(G.yRailF-G.yStand+4);STAND=document.createElement('canvas');STAND.width=Math.round(tw*DPR);STAND.height=Math.round(th*DPR);g=STAND.getContext('2d');g.scale(DPR,DPR);
  var bays=Math.max(4,Math.round(tw/160)),bw=tw/bays,seed=7;function rnd(){seed=(seed*9301+49297)%233280;return seed/233280;}
  for(var b=0;b<bays;b++){var x0=b*bw;
    if(b%3===1){/* grandstand bay with a roof */
      g.fillStyle='#5b3a26';g.fillRect(x0+bw*.04,th*.18,bw*.92,th*.82);g.fillStyle='#7a2e22';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x0,th*.2);g.lineTo(x0+bw*.5,th*.02);g.lineTo(x0+bw,th*.2);g.closePath();g.fill();
      g.fillStyle='#f2d27e';g.fillRect(x0+bw*.04,th*.19,bw*.92,th*.04);for(var c=0;c<4;c++){g.fillStyle='#3a2416';g.fillRect(x0+bw*(.08+c*.27),th*.23,bw*.04,th*.77);}
      g.fillStyle='rgba(20,12,8,.55)';g.fillRect(x0+bw*.04,th*.23,bw*.92,th*.77);
      g.fillStyle='#e11d48';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x0+bw*.5,th*.02);g.lineTo(x0+bw*.5,-th*.1);g.lineTo(x0+bw*.62,-th*.05);g.lineTo(x0+bw*.5,0);g.fill();
    } else {g.fillStyle='#6b4630';g.fillRect(x0,th*.42,bw,th*.58);g.fillStyle='#8a5a3a';for(var st=0;st<5;st++)g.fillRect(x0,th*(.45+st*.11),bw,2);}
    /* the crowd */
    var top=b%3===1?th*.3:th*.48;for(var rw=0;rw<6;rw++){var yy=top+rw*(th-top)/6.2;for(var px=x0+3;px<x0+bw-3;px+=Math.max(4,th*.07)){if(rnd()<.12)continue;
      var cols=['#f2d27e','#e2e8f0','#ef4444','#60a5fa','#34d399','#f472b6','#fb923c','#c4b5fd','#fde047'];g.fillStyle=cols[Math.floor(rnd()*cols.length)];
      var hs=Math.max(1.6,th*.035);g.beginPath();g.arc(px+rnd()*2,yy+rnd()*2,hs,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();g.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.35)';g.fillRect(px-hs,yy+hs*.6,hs*2,hs*1.6);}}}
  g.fillStyle='rgba(30,18,10,.25)';g.fillRect(0,th*.92,tw,th*.08);
  STAND.tw=tw;STAND.th=th;
  /* dirt texture tile */
  var dw=Math.max(256,Math.round(W*.5)),dh=Math.round(G.yRailN-G.yRailF);DIRT=document.createElement('canvas');DIRT.width=Math.round(dw*DPR);DIRT.height=Math.round(dh*DPR);g=DIRT.getContext('2d');g.scale(DPR,DPR);
  var dg=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,dh);dg.addColorStop(0,'#b07a4a');dg.addColorStop(.5,'#c08a55');dg.addColorStop(1,'#a8703f');g.fillStyle=dg;g.fillRect(0,0,dw,dh);
  seed=3;for(i=0;i<dw*dh/90;i++){var px2=rnd()*dw,py2=rnd()*dh;g.fillStyle=rnd()<.5?'rgba(90,55,25,.18)':'rgba(240,200,150,.16)';g.fillRect(px2,py2,1+rnd()*3,1);}
  for(i=0;i<NH+1;i++){var ly=dh*(G.y0-G.yRailF-G.sp*.5+i*G.sp)/dh;g.fillStyle='rgba(120,75,35,.16)';g.fillRect(0,ly,dw,1.5);}
  for(i=0;i<26;i++){var hx=rnd()*dw,hy=rnd()*dh;g.fillStyle='rgba(95,58,28,.28)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(hx,hy,3,1.4,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}
  DIRT.tw=dw;DIRT.th=dh;}
function mkLayer(seed,base,amp,col,par){var P=Math.max(600,W*1.4),mesas=[],x=0,r=seed*9301;function rnd(){r=(r*9301+49297)%233280;return r/233280;}
  while(x<P){var w=P*(0.05+rnd()*0.12),h=amp*(0.35+rnd()*0.9);mesas.push([x,w,h]);x+=w*(0.9+rnd()*1.2);}
  return {P:P,m:mesas,base:base,col:col,par:par};}
function drawLayer(l,off){var y0=l.base,o=((off*l.par)%l.P+l.P)%l.P;cx.fillStyle=l.col;
  for(var rep=-1;rep<2;rep++){var bx=rep*l.P-o;l.m.forEach(function(m){var x=bx+m[0],w=m[1],h=m[2];if(x>W||x+w<0)return;var sl=w*0.16;
    cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(x-sl,y0+2);cx.lineTo(x+sl*0.4,y0-h);cx.lineTo(x+w-sl*0.4,y0-h);cx.lineTo(x+w+sl,y0+2);cx.closePath();cx.fill();});}
  cx.fillRect(0,y0,W,G.yRailF-y0+1);}
function tile(img,tw,th,x,y,off,sw,sh){/* draw a horizontally repeating strip */var w=sw||tw,h=sh||th;var o=((off%w)+w)%w;for(var X=-o;X<W;X+=w)cx.drawImage(img,X,y,w+0.5,h);}

/* ---------- the horse (code art; HORSE_SHEET replaces it) ---------- */
function luma(c){var n=parseInt(c.slice(1),16);return ((n>>16)*.299+((n>>8)&255)*.587+(n&255)*.114)/255;}
function leg(g,jx,jy,a1,a2,l1,l2,col,hoof,w){var kx=jx+Math.sin(a1)*l1,ky=jy+Math.cos(a1)*l1,hx=kx+Math.sin(a2)*l2,hy=ky+Math.cos(a2)*l2;
  g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineCap='round';g.lineWidth=w;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(jx,jy);g.lineTo(kx,ky);g.stroke();g.lineWidth=w*.72;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(kx,ky);g.lineTo(hx,hy);g.stroke();
  g.fillStyle=hoof;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(hx+Math.sin(a2)*1.5,hy+1.2,3.4,2.4,a2,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}
function silkFill(g,h,path){g.save();path();g.clip();g.fillStyle=h.silk;g.fillRect(-120,-130,140,120);g.fillStyle=h.silk2;
  if(h.pat==='sash'){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-64,-90);g.lineTo(-56,-90);g.lineTo(-30,-62);g.lineTo(-38,-62);g.closePath();g.fill();}
  else if(h.pat==='hoops'){for(var y=-92;y<-60;y+=7)g.fillRect(-70,y,50,3.2);}
  else if(h.pat==='stars'){for(var i=0;i<6;i++){g.beginPath();g.arc(-56+(i%3)*8,-84+Math.floor(i/3)*9,2,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}}
  else if(h.pat==='halves'){g.fillRect(-48,-100,30,40);}
  else if(h.pat==='chevron'){g.lineWidth=3.5;g.strokeStyle=h.silk2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-64,-86);g.lineTo(-50,-74);g.lineTo(-36,-86);g.stroke();}
  g.restore();}
function drawHorse(x,y,s,h,phi,run,alpha,fr){
  if(IM.horse.ok)return drawHorseImg(x,y,s,h,phi,run,alpha);
  var g=cx;g.save();g.globalAlpha=alpha;g.translate(x,y);g.scale(s,s);
  var coat=h.coat[0],mane=h.coat[1],legc=h.coat[2],tau=Math.PI*2,bob=run?-2.4*Math.abs(Math.sin(phi*tau))+1.2:-0.5*Math.sin(phi*tau*0.5);
  /* shadow */
  g.fillStyle='rgba(40,20,8,.28)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-52,1,40,4,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
  g.translate(0,bob);
  function legA(o,front){var th=(phi+o)*tau;var a=run?(front?0.62:0.5)*Math.sin(th):(front?0.05:-0.06),lift=run?Math.max(0,Math.cos(th)):0;
    var a2=front?a-1.5*lift*run+(run?0:0):a+(run?(-0.25+0.95*lift):0.08);return [a,a2];}
  var far=['#00000033'];
  /* far legs */
  var lF=legA(.52,true),lH=legA(.08,false);
  leg(g,-30,-46,lF[0],lF[1],20,24,shade(legc,-.25),'#1a1310',6.4);leg(g,-76,-48,lH[0],lH[1],21,24,shade(legc,-.25),'#1a1310',7.2);
  /* tail */
  var tw=Math.sin(phi*tau)*(run?5:1.5);g.strokeStyle=mane;g.lineCap='round';g.lineWidth=5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-88,-62);g.quadraticCurveTo(-102,-60+tw,-104,-44+tw*1.4);g.stroke();
  g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-89,-60);g.quadraticCurveTo(-98,-52+tw,-99,-38+tw);g.stroke();
  /* body */
  var bg=g.createLinearGradient(0,-70,0,-36);bg.addColorStop(0,shade(coat,.18));bg.addColorStop(1,shade(coat,-.18));g.fillStyle=bg;
  g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-24,-56);g.bezierCurveTo(-28,-66,-36,-70,-46,-67);g.bezierCurveTo(-60,-64,-74,-70,-86,-64);g.bezierCurveTo(-94,-58,-92,-46,-84,-42);
  g.bezierCurveTo(-70,-37,-48,-36,-34,-38);g.bezierCurveTo(-26,-40,-21,-48,-24,-56);g.closePath();g.fill();
  /* neck + head, reaching with the stride */
  var nk=run?(-0.10+0.07*Math.sin(phi*tau+1)):0.04;g.save();g.translate(-34,-62);g.rotate(nk);g.translate(34,62);
  g.fillStyle=coat;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-40,-66);g.bezierCurveTo(-34,-80,-24,-88,-14,-90);g.lineTo(-6,-87);g.bezierCurveTo(-3,-82,1,-76,1,-72);g.bezierCurveTo(1,-68,-3,-66,-6,-67);
  g.bezierCurveTo(-10,-70,-14,-74,-18,-75);g.bezierCurveTo(-22,-68,-24,-60,-26,-52);g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.fillStyle=shade(coat,-.3);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-14,-90);g.lineTo(-12,-97);g.lineTo(-9,-89);g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.fillStyle='#120b07';g.beginPath();g.arc(-9.5,-83,1.5,0,tau);g.fill();g.beginPath();g.arc(-1,-71,1,0,tau);g.fill();
  if(h.coat===COATS[0]||h.coat===COATS[1]){g.fillStyle='#f5f0e6';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-8,-87);g.lineTo(-5,-86);g.lineTo(-1,-74);g.lineTo(-3,-74);g.closePath();g.fill();}
  g.strokeStyle=mane;g.lineWidth=4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-15,-89);g.bezierCurveTo(-24,-86,-34,-78,-42,-66);g.stroke();
  for(var m=0;m<5;m++){var mx=-18-m*5.2,my=-86+m*4.4;g.lineWidth=2.2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(mx,my);g.lineTo(mx-4-(run?3:1),my+2+(run?Math.sin(phi*tau+m)*2:1));g.stroke();}
  g.fillStyle='#2b1b10';g.fillRect(-11,-80,2,6);
  g.restore();
  /* near legs */
  var nF=legA(.42,true),nH=legA(0,false);
  leg(g,-76,-48,nH[0],nH[1],21,24,legc,'#100a07',7.4);leg(g,-30,-46,nF[0],nF[1],20,24,legc,'#100a07',6.6);
  /* saddle cloth with the number */
  var cl=CLOTH[(h.n-1)%CLOTH.length];g.fillStyle=cl[0];g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-66,-63);g.lineTo(-42,-63);g.lineTo(-44,-45);g.lineTo(-64,-45);g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.35)';g.lineWidth=1;g.stroke();g.fillStyle=cl[1];g.font='900 13px Inter,system-ui,sans-serif';g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='middle';g.fillText(String(h.n),-54,-53.5);
  g.fillStyle='#2b1a10';g.fillRect(-63,-66,20,4);
  /* reins */
  var jb=run?bob*-.5:0;g.strokeStyle='#2b1a10';g.lineWidth=1.2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-26,-76+jb);g.lineTo(-4,-70);g.stroke();
  /* jockey: crouched over the withers */
  g.save();g.translate(0,jb);
  g.strokeStyle='#f5f5f4';g.lineWidth=6;g.lineCap='round';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-58,-72);g.lineTo(-46,-66);g.stroke();
  g.strokeStyle='#1c1917';g.lineWidth=5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-46,-66);g.lineTo(-52,-57);g.stroke();
  silkFill(g,h,function(){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-62,-74);g.bezierCurveTo(-62,-84,-50,-90,-38,-88);g.lineTo(-34,-82);g.bezierCurveTo(-42,-78,-50,-72,-56,-68);g.closePath();});
  g.strokeStyle=h.silk;g.lineWidth=4.5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-40,-86);g.lineTo(-33,-78);g.lineTo(-26,-77);g.stroke();
  g.fillStyle='#f1c9a5';g.beginPath();g.arc(-30,-92,5,0,tau);g.fill();
  g.fillStyle=h.cap;g.beginPath();g.arc(-31,-93.5,5.4,Math.PI*1.02,Math.PI*2.05);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillRect(-27,-94.5,6,1.8);
  g.restore();
  g.restore();}
function shade(c,k){var n=parseInt(c.slice(1),16),r=n>>16,g=(n>>8)&255,b=n&255;if(k>0){r+=(255-r)*k;g+=(255-g)*k;b+=(255-b)*k;}else{r*=1+k;g*=1+k;b*=1+k;}return 'rgb('+Math.round(r)+','+Math.round(g)+','+Math.round(b)+')';}
var TINT={};
function drawHorseImg(x,y,s,h,phi,run,alpha){var img=IM.horse.img,fw=img.width/HORSE_FRAMES,fh=img.height,f=run?Math.floor(((phi%1)+1)%1*HORSE_FRAMES)%HORSE_FRAMES:0,dw=s*112,dh=dw*fh/fw;
  cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=alpha;cx.drawImage(img,f*fw,0,fw,fh,x-dw,y-dh,dw,dh);
  if(IM.silk.ok){var key=h.silk+'|'+f,t=TINT[key];if(!t){t=document.createElement('canvas');t.width=fw;t.height=fh;var tg=t.getContext('2d');tg.drawImage(IM.silk.img,f*fw,0,fw,fh,0,0,fw,fh);tg.globalCompositeOperation='source-in';tg.fillStyle=h.silk;tg.fillRect(0,0,fw,fh);TINT[key]=t;}
    cx.globalCompositeOperation='multiply';cx.drawImage(t,x-dw,y-dh,dw,dh);cx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';}
  /* saddle-cloth number */
  var cl=CLOTH[(h.n-1)%CLOTH.length];cx.fillStyle=cl[0];var bx=x-dw*.5,by=y-dh*.5;cx.fillRect(bx-s*9,by-s*7,s*18,s*14);cx.fillStyle=cl[1];cx.font='900 '+Math.round(s*12)+'px Inter,sans-serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.textBaseline='middle';cx.fillText(String(h.n),bx,by);
  cx.restore();}

/* ---------- camera + scene ---------- */
var V={lastT:0,parts:[],k:0,flash:0,photoAt:-1,hoofT:0,lastSec:-1,roared:-1,belled:-1};
var FX={reset:function(){V.parts=[];V.photoAt=-1;},load:function(){}};
function scene(t,ph){/* t = race seconds (<0 before the off). Returns positions and camera. */
  var xs=[],lead=0,back=1e9,i;
  for(i=0;i<NH;i++){var x=RM&&t>0?posAt(RM.H[i],t):0;xs.push(x);if(x>lead)lead=x;if(x<back)back=x;}
  var e=t<=0?0:Math.min(1,t/2.4),anc=G.anchorB+(G.anchor-G.anchorB)*e*e*(3-2*e);
  var camX=Math.min(lead,DIST+3),spread=Math.max(0.1,Math.min(lead,DIST)-back),avail=anc-W*.03-G.hl;
  var k=Math.max(0.5,Math.min(G.kT,avail/spread));
  return {xs:xs,camX:camX,k:k,lead:lead,anc:anc};}
function frame(now){
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  resize();if(!SKY||!G)return;var dt=Math.min(.05,(now-(V.lastT||now))/1000);V.lastT=now;
  var hn=hNow(),ph=R.ph,t=-99;
  if(R.id>=0&&ph!=='bet'&&ph!=='wait'&&RM)t=(hn-R.rs)*R.sp/1000;
  var frozen=false,showT=t;
  /* results: freeze on the winner hitting the wire (photo-finish frame) */
  if(RM&&t>RM.tl+TAIL_MS/1000){frozen=true;showT=RM.t1;}
  if(RM&&ph==='result'&&!frozen&&t>=RM.t1+0.3){frozen=true;showT=RM.t1;}
  if(ph==='bet'||!RM)showT=-99;
  var S=scene(showT>-50?showT:-1,ph);
  cx.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);
  drawBack(S);
  drawRunners(S,showT,dt,frozen);
  drawFront(S);
  drawHud(S,showT,frozen);
  if(frozen){if(V.photoAt!==R.id){V.photoAt=R.id;V.flash=1;}cx.fillStyle='rgba(60,40,20,.20)';cx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    var fx=S.anc+(DIST-S.camX)*S.k;cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.85)';cx.lineWidth=2;cx.setLineDash([6,5]);cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(fx,G.yRailF-8);cx.lineTo(fx,G.yRailN);cx.stroke();cx.setLineDash([]);
    if(!$('pod').classList.contains('on')){cx.font='400 '+Math.round(Math.max(14,Math.min(30,H*.07)))+'px Rye,Georgia,serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.textBaseline='middle';
      var lab=RM.gap<0.06?'PHOTO FINISH':'AT THE WIRE',lw=cx.measureText(lab).width+30;cx.fillStyle='rgba(10,14,26,.78)';rrect(W/2-lw/2,H*.16,lw,Math.max(26,H*.1),10);cx.fill();cx.fillStyle='#f2d27e';cx.fillText(lab,W/2,H*.16+Math.max(26,H*.1)/2+1);}}
  if(V.flash>0){cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(V.flash*.7)+')';cx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);V.flash=Math.max(0,V.flash-dt*3);}
  overlays(hn,t);
  sounds(t,dt);}
function rrect(x,y,w,h,r){cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(x+r,y);cx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);cx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);cx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);cx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);cx.closePath();}
function drawBack(S){
  var cam=S.camX;
  if(IM.bg.ok){var im=IM.bg.img,bh=G.yRailF,bw=bh*im.width/im.height;tile(im,0,0,0,0,cam*G.P*.06,bw,bh);}
  else{cx.drawImage(SKY,0,0,W,SKY.height/DPR);for(var li=0;li<LAY.length;li++)drawLayer(LAY[li],cam*G.P);}
  /* grandstand + crowd */
  if(BG_HAS_STAND&&IM.bg.ok){}
  else if(IM.stand.ok){var si=IM.stand.img,sh=G.yRailF-G.yStand,sw=sh*si.width/si.height;tile(si,0,0,0,G.yStand,cam*G.P*.45,sw,sh);}
  else{var off=cam*G.P*.45;var tw=STAND.tw,o=((off%tw)+tw)%tw;for(var X=-o;X<W;X+=tw)cx.drawImage(STAND,X,G.yStand,tw+.5,STAND.th);}
  /* the dirt */
  if(IM.dirt.ok){var di=IM.dirt.img,ds=Math.max(64,H*.35);cx.save();cx.beginPath();cx.rect(0,G.yRailF,W,G.yRailN-G.yRailF);cx.clip();var od=((cam*G.P*1.1)%ds+ds)%ds;
    for(var yy=G.yRailF;yy<G.yRailN;yy+=ds)for(var xx=-od;xx<W;xx+=ds)cx.drawImage(di,xx,yy,ds+.5,ds+.5);cx.restore();}
  else{var o2=((cam*G.P*1.1)%DIRT.tw+DIRT.tw)%DIRT.tw;for(var X2=-o2;X2<W;X2+=DIRT.tw)cx.drawImage(DIRT,X2,G.yRailF,DIRT.tw+.5,DIRT.th);}
  /* far rail */
  var rp=Math.max(46,H*.16),o3=((cam*G.P*.95)%rp+rp)%rp,ry=G.yRailF;
  cx.fillStyle='#e9e4da';for(var px=-o3;px<W+rp;px+=rp)cx.fillRect(px,ry-H*.045,Math.max(2,H*.008),H*.05);
  cx.fillStyle='#f8f5ef';cx.fillRect(0,ry-H*.048,W,Math.max(2,H*.009));cx.fillStyle='rgba(60,40,20,.25)';cx.fillRect(0,ry,W,2);
  /* distance poles on the far rail */
  for(var m=200;m<DIST;m+=200){var mx=S.anc+(m-cam)*S.k;if(mx<-20||mx>W+20)continue;cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.fillRect(mx-2,ry-H*.1,4,H*.1);cx.fillStyle=m%400===0?'#dc2626':'#16a34a';for(var q=0;q<3;q++)cx.fillRect(mx-2,ry-H*.1+q*H*.034,4,H*.017);}
  /* finish line + post */
  var fx=S.anc+(DIST-cam)*S.k;
  if(fx>-60&&fx<W+60){
    var cs=Math.max(4,G.sp*.22);cx.save();for(var yy2=G.yRailF,r=0;yy2<G.yRailN;yy2+=cs,r++){cx.fillStyle=r%2?'#111':'#f8fafc';cx.fillRect(fx-cs,yy2,cs,cs);cx.fillStyle=r%2?'#f8fafc':'#111';cx.fillRect(fx,yy2,cs,cs);}cx.restore();
    if(IM.post.ok){var pi=IM.post.img,phh=H*.42,pw=phh*pi.width/pi.height;cx.drawImage(pi,fx-pw/2,ry-phh,pw,phh);}
    else{cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.fillRect(fx-3,ry-H*.26,6,H*.26);cx.fillStyle='#dc2626';for(var q2=0;q2<5;q2++)cx.fillRect(fx-3,ry-H*.26+q2*H*.05,6,H*.025);
      var rr_=Math.max(9,H*.05);cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(fx,ry-H*.26-rr_*.6,rr_,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.strokeStyle='#dc2626';cx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,rr_*.25);cx.stroke();
      cx.fillStyle='#111';cx.font='900 '+Math.round(rr_*.62)+'px Inter,sans-serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.textBaseline='middle';cx.fillText('WIRE',fx,ry-H*.26-rr_*.6);}}}
function drawGate(S,part){var gx=S.anc+(0-S.camX)*S.k;if(gx<-G.hl*2||gx>W+G.hl*2)return;
  var open=0,hn=hNow();if(R.ph!=='bet'&&RM)open=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((hn-R.rs)*R.sp/1000)/0.35));
  var top=G.y0-G.hl*1.02,bot=G.y1+4,back=gx-G.hl*1.08,front=gx+G.hl*.06;
  if(IM.gate.ok){if(part==='front'){var gi=IM.gate.img,gh=bot-top+G.hl*.25,gw=gh*gi.width/gi.height;cx.drawImage(gi,front-gw,top-G.hl*.2,gw,gh);}return;}
  if(part==='back'){cx.fillStyle='#14532d';cx.fillRect(back-6,top,8,bot-top);cx.fillStyle='rgba(20,83,45,.35)';cx.fillRect(back,top,front-back,G.hl*.12);return;}
  if(part==='lane'){return;}
  /* front frame: tall posts, the top truss with the sign, the doors */
  cx.fillStyle='#166534';cx.fillRect(front,top-G.hl*.12,8,bot-top+G.hl*.12);cx.fillRect(back-6,top-G.hl*.12,front-back+14,G.hl*.14);
  cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.fillRect(back-6,top-G.hl*.12,front-back+14,G.hl*.03);
  var sw=Math.min(front-back,G.hl*1.1);cx.fillStyle='#7c2d12';rrect(back+(front-back-sw)/2,top-G.hl*.36,sw,G.hl*.22,6);cx.fill();cx.strokeStyle='#f2d27e';cx.lineWidth=2;cx.stroke();
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d27e';cx.font='400 '+Math.round(G.hl*.12)+'px Rye,Georgia,serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.textBaseline='middle';cx.fillText('DUSTY TRAIL',back+(front-back)/2,top-G.hl*.25);
  for(var l=0;l<NH;l++){var y=G.y0+l*G.sp,dh=G.hl*.62,sc=0.9+0.025*l;
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(22,101,52,.9)';var dw=G.hl*.12*(1-open);if(dw>0.5){cx.fillRect(front-dw+8,y-dh*sc,dw,dh*sc*.9);cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.fillRect(front-dw+8,y-dh*sc*.6,dw,2);}
    cx.fillStyle='#f2d27e';cx.font='800 '+Math.round(G.sp*.42)+'px Inter,sans-serif';cx.fillText(String(l+1),front+4,y-dh*sc-4);}}
function drawRunners(S,t,dt,frozen){
  var running=RM&&t>0&&!frozen;
  drawGate(S,'back');
  for(var l=0;l<NH;l++){var hh=F?F.h[l]:null;if(!hh)continue;var y=G.y0+l*G.sp,sc=0.9+0.025*l,x=S.anc+(S.xs[l]-S.camX)*S.k;
    var sp=RM&&t>0?speedAt(RM.H[l],t):0,run=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,sp/30)),phi=RM?(S.xs[l]/24+RM.H[l].ph):(l*.17);
    if(!RM||t<=0)phi=(performance.now()/1000*.25+l*.21)%1;
    /* dust */
    if(running&&run>.3&&!REDUCED&&Math.random()<dt*22*run){V.parts.push({x:S.xs[l]-G.hl*.82/S.k,y:y-2,vy:-rr(6,22),vx:rr(-1,1),r:rr(2,5)*G.hl/90,life:rr(.4,.8),age:0,ln:l});}
    drawParts(l,S,dt);
    if(x>-G.hl*.2&&x<W+G.hl*1.3)drawHorse(x,y,G.hl/100*sc,hh,phi,run,1);}
  drawGate(S,'front');}
function drawParts(l,S,dt){for(var i=V.parts.length-1;i>=0;i--){var p=V.parts[i];if(p.ln!==l)continue;p.age+=dt;
  if(p.age>=p.life){V.parts.splice(i,1);continue;}var f=1-p.age/p.life;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.r+=dt*8;var sx=S.anc+(p.x-S.camX)*S.k;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(196,150,100,'+(f*.5)+')';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,p.y,p.r,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();}
  if(V.parts.length>260)V.parts.splice(0,V.parts.length-260);}
function drawFront(S){
  var rp=Math.max(70,H*.26),o=((S.camX*G.P*1.45)%rp+rp)%rp,ry=G.yRailN;
  cx.fillStyle='#4d7c0f';cx.fillRect(0,ry+H*.01,W,H-ry);cx.fillStyle='#65a30d';cx.fillRect(0,ry+H*.01,W,Math.max(2,H*.012));
  cx.fillStyle='#f8f5ef';for(var px=-o;px<W+rp;px+=rp)cx.fillRect(px,ry-H*.035,Math.max(3,H*.013),H*.075);
  cx.fillRect(0,ry-H*.04,W,Math.max(3,H*.014));cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.18)';cx.fillRect(0,ry-H*.026,W,Math.max(1,H*.004));
  var fx=S.anc+(DIST-S.camX)*S.k;if(fx>-20&&fx<W+20){cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.fillRect(fx-3,ry-H*.09,6,H*.13);cx.fillStyle='#dc2626';cx.fillRect(fx-3,ry-H*.09,6,H*.025);}}
function drawHud(S,t,frozen){if(!F)return;
  /* track map: the whole trip with everyone's position */
  var x0=12,x1=W-12,y=10;cx.fillStyle='rgba(10,14,26,.55)';rrect(x0-6,y-6,x1-x0+12,14,7);cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.25)';cx.fillRect(x0,y,x1-x0,2);cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.fillRect(x1-1,y-4,2,10);
  for(var m=200;m<DIST;m+=200){cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.4)';cx.fillRect(x0+(x1-x0)*m/DIST,y-2,1,6);}
  var ord=liveOrder(S,t,frozen);
  for(var j=ord.length-1;j>=0;j--){var n=ord[j],hh=F.h[n-1],px=x0+(x1-x0)*Math.min(1,S.xs[n-1]/DIST);cx.fillStyle=hh.silk;cx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.6)';cx.lineWidth=1.5;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,y+1,5.5,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();}
  if(t<=0&&!frozen)return;
  /* running order down the right side */
  var cw=G.colW,rh=G.narrow?21:24,cxp=W-cw-8,cy=26;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(10,14,26,.62)';rrect(cxp-4,cy-4,cw+8,rh*NH+8+(G.narrow?0:16),9);cx.fill();
  if(!G.narrow){cx.fillStyle='#cbb07a';cx.font='800 9.5px Inter,sans-serif';cx.textAlign='left';cx.textBaseline='middle';cx.fillText(frozen?'FINAL':'POSITIONS',cxp+2,cy+5);
    cx.textAlign='right';cx.fillStyle='#e2e8f0';cx.fillText(Math.max(0,Math.min(t,RM?RM.t1:t)).toFixed(1)+'s',cxp+cw,cy+5);cy+=16;}
  ord.forEach(function(n,i){var hh=F.h[n-1],yy=cy+i*rh;
    if(!G.narrow){cx.fillStyle='#94a3b8';cx.font='800 10px Inter,sans-serif';cx.textAlign='left';cx.textBaseline='middle';cx.fillText(String(i+1),cxp+2,yy+rh/2);}
    var bx=G.narrow?cxp+2:cxp+14,bs=rh-4;cx.fillStyle=hh.silk;rrect(bx,yy+2,bs,bs,4);cx.fill();cx.fillStyle=hh.silk2;cx.fillRect(bx+bs*.42,yy+2,bs*.18,bs);
    cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.font='900 '+Math.round(bs*.6)+'px Inter,sans-serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.lineWidth=2.5;cx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.75)';cx.strokeText(String(n),bx+bs/2,yy+rh/2+.5);cx.fillText(String(n),bx+bs/2,yy+rh/2+.5);
    if(!G.narrow){cx.fillStyle='#f8fafc';cx.font='700 11px Inter,sans-serif';cx.textAlign='left';var nm=hh.name;while(cx.measureText(nm).width>cw-bs-22&&nm.length>3)nm=nm.slice(0,-1);if(nm!==hh.name)nm=nm.replace(/\s+$/,'')+'.';cx.fillText(nm,bx+bs+5,yy+rh/2+.5);}});}
function liveOrder(S,t,frozen){var a=[1,2,3,4,5,6];if(frozen&&RM)return RM.ord.slice();if(!RM||t<=0)return a;
  return a.sort(function(x,y){var dx=Math.min(S.xs[y-1],DIST+0)-Math.min(S.xs[x-1],DIST+0);if(Math.abs(dx)<1e-6){var tx=RM.H[x-1].T,ty=RM.H[y-1].T;if(S.xs[x-1]>=DIST&&S.xs[y-1]>=DIST)return tx-ty;}return dx||x-y;});}

var cmtIdx=-1,lastMx='';
function overlays(hn,t){
  var mx=$('mx'),lab='',big='',sm='',show=false;
  if(R.ph==='bet'&&R.id>=0){var left=Math.max(0,R.be-hn),tot=BET_MS/R.sp,sec=Math.ceil(left/1000);show=true;lab='Post time';big=Math.floor(sec/60)+':'+('0'+sec%60).slice(-2);sm='Race '+R.id+' \u00b7 6 runners \u00b7 1,000 m';
    $('cdi').style.transform='scaleX('+Math.max(0,Math.min(1,left/tot)).toFixed(3)+')';if(sec!==V.lastSec){V.lastSec=sec;if(sec<=5&&sec>0)SFX.tick();}
    if(left<=0){lab='Betting closed';big='0:00';}}
  else if(R.ph==='race'&&t<0){show=true;lab='They\u2019re at the gate';big=Math.max(0,-t).toFixed(1)+'s';sm='Race '+R.id;$('cdi').style.transform='scaleX(0)';}
  else if(R.id<0){show=true;lab='Saddling up';big='\u2026';sm='';}
  var k=show+lab+big+sm;if(k!==lastMx){lastMx=k;mx.classList.toggle('off',!show);mx.classList.toggle('hot',R.ph==='bet'&&R.be-hn<10000);$('mxs').textContent=lab;$('mxb').textContent=big;$('mxm').textContent=sm;}
  /* the race caller */
  var c=$('cmt');
  if(RM&&R.ph!=='bet'){var ev=RM.cmt,i=-1;for(var j=0;j<ev.length;j++)if(ev[j].t<=t)i=j;
    if(i!==cmtIdx){cmtIdx=i;if(i>=0){$('cmtT').textContent=ev[i].x;c.classList.remove('off');c.classList.remove('flash');void c.offsetWidth;c.classList.add('flash');}else c.classList.add('off');}
    if($('pod').classList.contains('on'))c.classList.add('off');}
  else if(cmtIdx!==-2){cmtIdx=-2;c.classList.add('off');}
  lockUI();}
function sounds(t,dt){if(!AC||!opt.sound||away||!RM||R.ph==='bet')return;
  if(V.belled!==R.id&&t>=0&&t<1){V.belled=R.id;SFX.bell();}
  if(t>0&&t<RM.tl){V.hoofT-=dt;if(V.hoofT<=0){V.hoofT=.16;SFX.hoof(t<RM.t1?1:.6);}}
  if(V.roared!==R.id&&t>=RM.t1-2.4&&t<RM.t1){V.roared=R.id;SFX.roar();}}

/* keyboard: 1-6 bet or pick a post; W P S A (across) E (exacta) Q T U (superfecta) D switch type; B box, K key; Enter places an exotic */
addEventListener('keydown',function(e){var tg=document.activeElement&&document.activeElement.tagName;if(tg==='INPUT'||tg==='TEXTAREA'||tg==='SELECT'||e.repeat||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.altKey)return;
  var k=e.key.toUpperCase();if(/^[1-6]$/.test(k)){e.preventDefault();ac();var n=+k;if(exo(ty)||ty==='D'){if(ty==='D'&&sel.length){dd2=n;boardKey='';renderBoard();}else tapPick(n);}else placeBet(ty,'st',[n]);return;}
  var map={W:'W',P:'P',S:'S',A:'A',E:'X',Q:'Q',T:'T',U:'F',D:'D'};
  if(map[k]){setTy(map[k]);return;}
  if(k==='B'&&BT[ty].box){bmode='box';boardKey='';renderBoard();}else if(k==='K'&&BT[ty].key){bmode='key';boardKey='';renderBoard();}
  else if(k==='ENTER'){var g=$('exGo');if(g&&!g.disabled)g.click();}});

function seg(id,fn){var el=$(id);el.addEventListener('click',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;[].forEach.call(el.children,function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b);});fn(b.getAttribute('data-v'));});}

var settled=false;
function decide(){
  var r=roster(),want=opt.live&&L&&L.available()&&L.status==='live'&&r.length>=2&&!away&&settled;
  var nm=!want?'solo':(L.isHost()?'host':'view');
  if(nm!==mode){
    /* finish a race we have money on before moving to someone else's track */
    if(nm==='view'&&mode!=='view'&&MB.some(function(b){return b.st==='live';})){clearTimeout(decide.t);decide.t=setTimeout(decide,500);return;}
    var was=mode;mode=nm;
    if(nm==='view'){stopEngine();MB.forEach(function(b){if(b.st==='placed')refund(b,'Moved to the live track: stake returned');});R.ph='wait';R.id=-1;RM=null;PL=[];podHide();renderPlayers();renderSlip();lastFrom=null;try{L.send('hello',{});}catch(e){}}
    else if(was==='view'||!E){eStart(was==='view'?lastSt:null);}
    try{if(CH)CH.refresh();}catch(e){}
  }
  liveUI();ui();
}
function liveUI(){
  var s=L?L.status:'off',avail=L&&L.available(),r=roster();
  $('liveDot').className='ldot '+(!avail||!opt.live?'off':s);
  $('liveTxt').textContent=!avail?'Offline (live tracks are not set up)':!opt.live?'Off: betting with the computer players':s==='live'?(r.length>1?'Live at Track '+L.table+(mode==='host'?' \u00b7 you run the starting gate':''):'Live at Track '+L.table+' \u00b7 waiting for bettors'):s==='connecting'?'Connecting\u2026':s==='error'?'Could not connect. Racing solo.':'Offline';
  $('livePeople').innerHTML=r.length?r.map(function(p){return '<span class="lp'+(p.isMe?' me':'')+'">'+esc(p.name)+(p.isHost?' <i>host</i>':'')+(p.isMe?' <i>you</i>':'')+'</span>';}).join(''):'<span class="lp">Nobody else here right now. Anyone who opens Dusty Trail Derby joins your track.</span>';
  if(L&&document.activeElement!==$('liveName'))$('liveName').value=L.me.name;
  var lc=$('liveChip');lc.classList.toggle('hide',mode==='solo');lc.textContent='LIVE \u00b7 '+r.length+(r.length===1?' player':' players');
}

Across the board if you want to sleep at night.':'Across the board on the favorite, partner.','Longshots pay big, but they are longshots for a reason.','Box three in the trifecta and let them sort it out.']);},

}

<\u002fscript>

<script>
(function(){
  var last=0;
  var sent=document.createElement('div');
  sent.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  sent.style.cssText='height:0;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;clear:both';
  function measure(){
    if(!document.body) return 0;
    if(sent.parentNode!==document.body) document.body.appendChild(sent);
    var pb=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).paddingBottom)||0;
    return Math.ceil(sent.getBoundingClientRect().bottom+(window.pageYOffset||0)+pb);
  }
  function post(){
    var h=measure();
    if(!h||Math.abs(h-last)<2) return;
    last=h;
    try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'h',h:h},'*');}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('load',post); addEventListener('resize',post);
  if(window.ResizeObserver){
    try{ var ro=new ResizeObserver(post); ro.observe(document.body);
         var w=document.querySelector('.wrap'); if(w) ro.observe(w); }catch(e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('load',post,true);
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible'&&e.data.on){last=0;setTimeout(post,30);}});
  setInterval(post,900); post();
})();
